### [1] [AI Acceleration of Muon Collider R&D: A Report from the AI4MuC Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14303)
*J. Scott Berg,David Bizzozero,Tulika Bose,Lance Cooley,John Dervan,Auralee Edelen,Peter Elmer,Cristiano Fanelli,Tova Holmes,Walter Hopkins,Sergo Jindariani,Michael Kagan,Maira Khan,Young-Kee Kim,Matt LeBlanc,Lawrence Lee,Matthias Liepe,Radha Mastandrea,Patrick Meade,Federico Meloni,Chad Mitchell,Linh Nguyen,Isobel Ojalvo,Ryan Roberts,Nicholas Smith,Jean-Luc Vay,Gordon Watts,Drew Winder,Chenran Xu,Katsuya Yonehara*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文是AI4MuC研讨会的总结报告，梳理了人工智能在Muon Collider研发各物理瓶颈环节中的应用场景，指出AI在高维设计空间、昂贵模拟、子系统耦合权衡及工作流优化方面具有特殊适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon Collider是实现多TeV轻子碰撞最具吸引力的途径之一，但需要加速器与探测器界解决一整套高度耦合、无现成运行经验可循的难题。缪子须在衰变前完成产生、捕获、冷却、加速与碰撞，探测器还须在束线衰变产物主导的本底环境中提取干净物理信号。AI虽不能替代加速器物理、探测器设计或工程验证，却与若干核心障碍高度契合，因此有必要系统梳理AI的潜在应用场景以加速研发进程。

Method: 将AI4MuC研讨会内容凝练为AI用例总结报告，围绕关键物理瓶颈进行组织与分类，包括缪子产生与捕获、电离冷却、磁体与射频技术、束流诱导本底下的探测器设计、本底模拟与生成、重建以及软件等环节。

Result: 报告识别出AI在Muon Collider R&D中具有改变研发节奏潜力的若干应用方向，并按上述物理瓶颈领域进行了系统归纳，明确了AI适用于高维设计空间探索、昂贵模拟加速、耦合子系统权衡以及当前过慢或过于脆弱的工作流等场景。

Conclusion: AI并非传统加速器物理、探测器设计与工程验证的替代品，而是与Muon Collider面临的核心障碍高度匹配的加速工具，通过围绕各物理瓶颈领域部署AI用例，有望显著加快Muon Collider的研发进程。

Abstract: A Muon Collider is one of the most attractive routes to multi-TeV lepton collisions, but it requires the accelerator and detector communities to solve a tightly coupled set of problems that have no close precedent in an operating collider. Muons must be produced, captured, cooled, accelerated, and collided before they decay. The detector must then extract clean physics from an environment dominated by decay products from the beamline. Artificial intelligence is not a substitute for accelerator physics, detector design, or engineering validation. It is, however, unusually well matched to several of the central obstacles: high-dimensional design spaces, expensive simulations, coupled subsystem trade-offs, and workflows that are currently too slow or too fragile to support broad design exploration. This report distills the AI4MuC workshop into a summary of AI use cases with the potential to change the pace of Muon Collider R&D, organized around the following physics bottlenecks: muon production and capture, ionization cooling, magnet and RF technology, detector design in beam-induced background, background simulation and generation, reconstruction, and software.

</details>


### [2] [Learn the Dynamics, Enforce the Physics: Generative Latent Operators for High-Dimensional Beam Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14537)
*Haohan Zou,Alexander Scheinker,Yuanyuan Shi*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: GLOBE：物理约束的生成式潜算子，用于3D束流-场演化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIC模拟是加速器束流动力学建模的必备工具，但计算成本高，限制了配置探索、虚拟诊断与控制器设计等需要大量查询的工作流。

Method: 将束流-场状态压缩至紧凑潜空间，演化分解为两部分：控制条件神经算子学习主导动力学，幅度归一化生成模型捕捉残差分布以恢复细尺度结构；并通过分区域无参数算子施加场约束（静电区用Poisson求解重构电场，电磁区用谱投影施加高斯定律与无散磁场）。

Result: 在束团束流传输与等离子体尾场加速两个高分辨率3D基准上，相对最强基线DPOT，相对L2误差分别降低9%与13%，低频谱误差降低69%与51%，中频谱误差降低54%与20%，满足场约束，并将数小时PIC模拟缩短至每次rollout一分钟。

Conclusion: GLOBE以物理约束的生成式潜算子高效、准确地建模大规模3D束流-场演化，显著降低计算成本并保持物理一致性。

Abstract: Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulation is indispensable for modeling beam dynamics in particle accelerators, but its computational cost limits many-query workflows, such as configuration exploration, virtual diagnostics, and controller design. We introduce GLOBE, a physics-constrained Generative Latent Operator for Beam Evolution, modeling the nonlinear, control-dependent, multiscale evolution of 3D beam--field dynamics covering $10^7$ spatial cells. GLOBE compresses beam--field states into a compact latent space and factors their evolution into two components. A control-conditioned neural operator learns the dominant dynamics, while an amplitude-normalized generative model captures the residual distribution, recovering fine-scale structures and conditional variations. GLOBE enforces field constraints by regime-specific, parameter-free operators: a Poisson solve reconstructs the electric field in the electrostatic regime, and a spectral projection imposes Gauss's law and a divergence-free magnetic field in the electromagnetic regime. On two high-resolution 3D particle-accelerator benchmarks, bunched-beam transport and plasma-wakefield acceleration, GLOBE lowers relative-$L_2$ error by 9\% and 13\%, low-frequency spectral error by 69\% and 51\%, and mid-frequency spectral error by 54\% and 20\% relative to the strongest baseline (DPOT), satisfies field constraints, and reduces hours of PIC simulation to a minute per rollout.

</details>


### [3] [Multi-Objective Design of C-Shaped Targets for Energy--Divergence Control in Laser-Driven Proton Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14824)
*Mohammad Rezaei-Pandari,Mahdi Shayganmanesh,Mohammad Hossein Mahdieh*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究结合二维粒子模拟与约束多目标贝叶斯优化，对C形靶激光驱动质子进行能量-发散权衡优化，获得15个Pareto最优设计，并揭示其物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光驱动质子源在能量与角度质量之间存在固有折衷，需要明确的设计路径来同时优化截止能量与发散度，并满足转换效率约束。

Method: 采用二维粒子模拟（PIC）结合约束多目标贝叶斯优化，改变5个几何与聚焦参数，在质子转换效率η_H≥9%约束下，最大化质子截止能量并最小化E_k≥5 MeV前向质子的RMS发散度；辅以匹配阶段诊断与数值检验。

Result: 82个设计中60个可行、15个Pareto最优。最大能量端点：28.64 MeV、52.51°；最小发散端点：11.47 MeV、11.13°；代表性折衷：21.44 MeV、38.44°。诊断将其关联到光场累积、电子加速、电荷分离与外鞘组织。反射时序不支持单调俘获时长规律，电荷鞘关系仍为非局域。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一条设计分辨的路径，用于同时调控激光驱动质子的能量与角度质量，并澄清了相关加速机制的非局域性与非单调性。

Abstract: We optimize the energy-divergence trade-off of laser-driven protons from a C-shaped target using two-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations and constrained multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Five geometry and focusing parameters are varied to maximize proton cutoff energy and minimize the RMS divergence of forward protons with E_k >= 5 MeV under a proton-only conversion-efficiency constraint of eta_H >= 9%. Of 82 designs, 60 are feasible and 15 Pareto-optimal. The Max-Energy and Min-Divergence endpoints yield 28.64 MeV at 52.51 degrees and 11.47 MeV at 11.13 degrees, respectively; a representative compromise yields 21.44 MeV at 38.44 degrees. Matched-stage diagnostics link these regimes to optical-field buildup, electron energization, charge separation, and outer-sheath organization. Numerical tests support the principal trends. Reflection timing does not support a monotonic trapping-duration law, and the charge-sheath relation remains non-local. These results establish a design-resolved route to controlling proton energy and angular quality.

</details>


### [4] [Brilliance: toward autonomous computational research in particle accelerator physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15615)
*Jinyu Wan,Yu Zhao,Wei Bao,Ying Li,Hao Zhong,Xiaohan Lu,Yi Jiao*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 提出Brilliance智能体，结合大语言模型、可复用物理知识与数值模拟，在加速器物理中自主开展自适应研究，可设计第四代同步辐射光源并标定运行光源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理计算研究需要模拟与科学解释持续交互，依赖大量专家投入，成本高、难以规模化，因此希望将长期科学探究任务委托给具备领域知识与数值工具的智能体。

Method: 构建Brilliance智能体：集成大语言模型、可复用的物理知识库与数值模拟工具；仅凭单一用户提示即可自主设计实验方案，并根据数值证据自适应地修正计算策略。

Result: 能够设计满足耦合发射度与非线性接受度要求的第四代同步辐射光源；进一步在真实工程约束下得到发射度低于10 pm且动态孔径较大的设计方案，指向硬X射线衍射极限运行路径，但动量接受度目标尚未达成；同时可对运行中光源模型进行标定，依据实测响应准确推断其磁格与标定误差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将持续的科学探究任务委托给具备领域专长与数值工具的智能体是可行路径，为加速器物理及其他计算物理领域的自主研究提供了范式。

Abstract: Computational research in physics requires continuous interplay between simulation and scientific interpretation, demanding substantial specialist effort. Here we introduce Brilliance, a research agent that combines large language models, reusable physics knowledge and numerical simulation to conduct adaptive investigations in accelerator physics. Given a single user prompt, Brilliance designs a fourth-generation synchrotron light source that meets coupled emittance and nonlinear-acceptance requirements. Further exploration yields a design combining sub-10-pm emittance with a large dynamic aperture under realistic engineering constraints, suggesting a route towards hard-X-ray diffraction-limited operation, although the momentum-acceptance target remains unmet. Brilliance also calibrates a model of an operating light source against measured responses, accurately inferring its lattice and calibration errors. Across these studies, Brilliance autonomously revises its computational strategies in response to numerical evidence. These results demonstrate a route towards delegating sustained scientific investigations to agents equipped with domain expertise and numerical tools.

</details>


### [5] [Anomaly Detection in Bunch Oscillations for Early Warning of Sudden Beam Loss at SuperKEKB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17241)
*H. Haigh,P. Horak,G. Inguglia,C. Krause,G. Mitsuka,H. Nakayama,R. Nomaru,M. Tobiyama*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用SuperKEKB储存环中Bunch Oscillation Recorder(BOR)记录的横向束团振荡，构建并比较多种基于振荡的异常检测算法，证明束团振荡可作为比现有电荷式探测器更早的紧急停机(abort)触发信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SuperKEKB的突发束损(SBL)事件会在几次回旋内损失大量储存束流，其时标与现有abort系统20-30 μs的延迟相当或更短，已损坏准直器并威胁Belle II子探测器。因此需要在束流真正丢失之前找到可提前触发的预警信号。

Method: 作者分析2024年运行期低能环记录的208例SBL事件，提取每次回旋的横向束团位置(来自BOR)。方法涵盖从简单滚动统计量到以预测束团振荡为目标的机器学习模型（包括DeepLSTM），并与模拟现有abort传感器的电荷基准方法进行对比；在未用于训练/校准的173个事件上进行评估。

Result: 在173个测试事件中，表现最均衡的模型(DeepLSTM)在5个训练随机种子下于86-90%的事件中先于电荷基准触发，平均提前时间为25-30 μs。唯一的误报触发发生在束流丢失前约60回旋开始并持续至束损的振荡上，更像是早期前兆而非虚假触发。

Conclusion: 束团振荡是一种可行的早期abort触发依据。作者建议进行影子模式(shadow-mode)部署，以在连续运行中测量真实误报率并进一步验证该方案。

Abstract: Sudden beam loss (SBL) events at the SuperKEKB collider result in the loss of a large fraction of the stored beam within just a few revolutions, on a timescale comparable to or shorter than the 20-30 μs latency of the existing abort system. Such events have damaged collimators and threaten Belle II subdetectors. Bunch Oscillation Recorders (BORs) capture transverse bunch positions at each revolution, which often show sizable oscillations that precede significant beam current loss. We test whether these precursors can be detected earlier than a charge-based reference that mimics the existing abort sensors. We apply several oscillation-based anomaly detection algorithms to 208 SBL events recorded in the low-energy ring during the 2024 run period, ranging from simple rolling statistics to machine learning models trained to forecast bunch oscillation. On 173 events not used for training or calibration, the best-balanced model (DeepLSTM) triggers before the charge-based reference in 86-90% of events across five training seeds, with a mean lead time of 25-30 μs. The only trigger classified as a false alarm fires on an oscillation that begins about 60 turns before the abort and persists until the beam is lost, making it more likely an early precursor than a spurious trigger. These results establish bunch oscillations as a viable early abort trigger and motivate a shadow-mode deployment to measure the false-alarm rate in continuous operation.

</details>


### [6] [HOM Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17293)
*Nicoleta Baboi*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本讲座综述了加速结构中高阶模（HOM）的抑制方法，包括避免其产生、减小其效应以及将其信号用于束流与结构诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOM是带电粒子束激发并遗留的谐振电磁场，会严重劣化束流品质（如多束团能量散度与横向发射度），因此必须加以研究和抑制。

Method: 综述多种HOM抑制途径：一是通过优化加速结构与阻尼系统设计避免HOM存在；二是利用快速反馈或束流对准等手段减小已激发HOM场的影响。

Result: 归纳了避免HOM产生与削弱HOM效应两类抑制策略，并指出可将HOM信号用于束流或加速结构诊断。

Conclusion: HOM抑制需结合结构设计、阻尼系统及束流控制手段，同时HOM信号本身亦可转化为有用的诊断工具。

Abstract: Higher Order Modes (HOMs) in accelerating structures are resonant electromagnetic fields excited and left behind by charged particle bunches. They can severely degrade the beam quality in accelerators, such as the multi-bunch energy spread and transverse emittance, and therefore one must carefully study and mitigate them. In this lecture, various methods of HOM mitigation are reviewed. On one hand, one tries to avoid their existence, for example by careful design of the accelerating structures and damping systems. On the other hand, one can reduce the effect of the excited HOM fields, for example by means of fast feedbacks or beam alignment. Additionally, a few ways to make HOM signals useful are referred to, such as diagnostics of the beam or of accelerating structures.

</details>


### [7] [A modern particle-in-cell code for beam-beam simulations of future colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17557)
*Arianna Formenti,Remi Lehe,Axel Huebl,Stepan S. Bulanov,Jens Osterhoff,Andrew Myers,Weiqun Zhang,Spencer Gessner,Peter Kicsiny,Anna Kinderman,Bao Nguyen,Jinseo Park,Jean-Luc Vay*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入开源PIC代码WarpX作为下一代轻子对撞机束束相互作用的建模工具，并在宽束流参数范围内与GUINEA-PIG和CAIN进行基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代对撞机设计需准确预测束束相互作用效应（强聚焦力、辐射效应、背景粒子产生）并据此优化机器性能；解析公式仅提供定性认识，这些非线性耦合效应需数值方法精确描述，而现有基准代码缺乏现代GPU可移植性与可扩展性。

Method: 将开源粒子-in-cell代码WarpX（v26.09）应用于对撞机相互作用点建模，在分布式GPU架构上高性能运行；开展收敛性扫描，并在对应不同对撞机概念的宽束流参数范围内，针对光度谱、光子发射和次级粒子产生，与已确立的束束代码GUINEA-PIG（v1.2.2）和CAIN（v2.42）进行基准对比。

Result: WarpX可复现基准代码的束束可观测量（光度谱、光子发射、次级粒子产生），同时支持GPU可移植、可扩展的仿真，并在收敛性扫描中表现一致。

Conclusion: 结果确立WarpX为面向未来对撞机研究、可扩展的高保真束束建模范台。

Abstract: A key challenge in the design of next-generation colliders is predicting the effects of beam-beam interactions and using these predictions to optimize machine performance. Beam-beam interactions are characterized by strong focusing forces, radiative effects, and the generation of background particles. While analytical formulas provide insight into the dynamics of beam-beam interactions, these nonlinear, coupled effects are most accurately described with numerical methods. In this paper, we introduce the open-source particle-in-cell code WarpX as a modern tool for modeling the interaction point in future lepton colliders. WarpX provides high-performance execution on distributed GPU architectures within an open-source and actively maintained code base. We present convergence scans and benchmarks against the established beam-beam codes GUINEA-PIG (v1.2.2) and CAIN (v2.42) for luminosity spectra, photon emission, and secondary-particle production under a wide range of beam parameters corresponding to different collider concepts. Our results show that WarpX (v26.09) reproduces the benchmark beam-beam observables while enabling GPU-portable, extensible simulations. These results establish WarpX as a scalable platform for high-fidelity beam-beam modeling in future collider studies.

</details>


### [8] [Phase Memory Controls Radiative Depolarization Near an Isolated Spin Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18397)
*Zhe Duan*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立统一有限记忆响应理论，定量描述电子储存环中辐射退极化在关联边带理论与局部共振密度理论之间的过渡区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辐射退极化理论分为关联边带理论与局部共振密度理论两类，但两者之间交叉过渡区的定量描述长期缺失。

Method: 从孤立共振附近的随机自旋动力学出发，通过分离相干共振贡献与随机涨落，推导出连通关联函数形式，并用累积共振相对自旋相位的连通关联确定偏心弱共振退极化率。

Result: 两种传统理论均作为该响应的受控渐近极限出现；理论同时捕捉主导交叉的残余同步相位记忆；蒙特卡洛自旋跟踪在整个交叉区验证了预测速率，并重现了复发诱导的广义边带结构。

Conclusion: 连通关联公式为将辐射退极化理论推广到更一般的储存环随机自旋-轨道动力学提供了基础。

Abstract: Radiative depolarization near spin resonances in electron storage rings is conventionally described by correlated-sideband and local resonance-density theories, but a quantitative description of the crossover between them has remained lacking. Here we derive a unified finite-memory response from stochastic spin dynamics near an isolated resonance. By separating the coherent resonance contribution from stochastic fluctuations, we show that the connected correlation of the accumulated resonance-relative spin phase determines the leading off-center weak-resonance depolarization rate. Both conventional theories emerge as controlled asymptotic limits of this response, which also captures the surviving synchrotron phase memory governing the crossover. Monte Carlo spin tracking validates the predicted rates throughout the crossover and reproduces the recurrence-induced generalized-sideband structure. More broadly, the connected-correlation formulation provides a basis for extending radiative depolarization theory to more general stochastic spin-orbit dynamics in storage rings.

</details>

### [9] [Additional ionization during nuclear $β^{\pm}$ decay in atomic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14109)
*Alexei M. Frolov*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 提出计算原子核β±衰变过程中原子束缚–自由激发概率的原创数值方法，推导并验证少电子原子体系各类激发末态概率的闭式解析公式，属该问题的首个非近似、系统性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核β±衰变会伴随原子电子态的改变，其束缚–自由激发概率需要精确计算；此前研究多为近似处理或不够完整，缺乏非近似且系统的理论与计算方法。

Method: 建立原创的数值计算方法；针对实际原子与离子中的束缚–自由激发，推导少电子原子体系不同激发情形下末态概率的闭合解析公式，并对这些公式进行验证。

Abstract: Optical lattice clocks in space could improve satellite navigation systems and enable new tests of fundamental physics, including gravitational-wave detection and tests of relativity. Abstract: Rydberg atoms occupy a central role in quantum science; their loosely bound outer electron gives rise to properties that enable quantum information processing1 or the formation of exotic molecular bonds2. Beyond these roles, they have been proposed as a neutral platform for the observation of quantum effects in collisions and reactions of the ionic core with neutral particles3, 4, a long-standing goal of ion-based chemistry and physics. However, all of the experimental results so far could be explained using a classical description of the internuclear motion5. Here we demonstrate cold collisions inside a Rydberg helium atom, achieved by merging helium Rydberg and ground-state helium beams and imaging individual scattering events. We resolve the fully quantum-coherent motion and observe matter-wave diffraction and tunnelling-driven shape resonances down to collision energies of 90 mK. In the case of identical helium nuclei, we demonstrate a new regime of identical-particle Mott interference6, where quantum statistics begin to govern the total scattering cross section. For distinct isotopes, we show the breakdown of the Born-Oppenheimer approximation and benchmark parameter-free, state-of-the-art theory. We directly measure the helium isotope ionization-energy difference to be 15.0 +/- 0.1 K. Our experiments establish Rydberg atoms as a platform for the direct observation of quantum dynamics with a general class of ions, atoms and molecules.

Method: 采用密度矩阵形式（density matrix formalism）与不可约张量算符代数（algebra of irreducible tensor operators）进行理论推导。计算针对泵浦-探测（pump-probe）机制，在存在更强反向传播光束的条件下求解探测光束的吸收系数；推导过程对激光束偏振参数的任意取值、原子光学偶极跃迁的任意角动量以及多种弛豫过程均保持普遍性。

Result: 获得了探测光束吸收系数的解析表达式，适用于任意偏振参数、任意光学偶极跃迁角动量和多种弛豫过程。该理论能够同时正确计入多种非线性光学效应，并可定量分析各效应对整体共振谱线的部分贡献。分析进一步预测：相比实践中常用的正交线偏振反向光束配置，采用具有相反圆偏振的反向光束可获得更大的亚多普勒共振幅度。

Conclusion: 所建立的理论克服了简化三能级模型的局限，可完整反映原子真实能级结构下多种非线性光学效应的共同作用，并为微型光频段量子频率标准中亚多普勒共振的优化提供了理论依据，特别是揭示了反向圆偏振配置在增强共振幅度方面的优势。

Abstract: Sub-Doppler resonances observed in miniature cells ($V \ll 1 cm^3$) with Rb or Cs atoms in the field of counterpropagating bichromatic laser beams are of interest for the creation of a miniature quantum frequency standard (QFS) of the optical range. However, a theory of these resonances that takes into full account the real structure of energy levels in an atom has not yet been constructed, which would make it possible to obtain explicit analytical expressions for the absorption coefficient of the medium. This paper addresses this gap using the density matrix formalism and the algebra of irreducible tensor operators. Namely, we obtained an analytical expression for the absorption coefficient of the probe beam in the presence of a more intense counterpropagating beam (pump-probe regime). Moreover, the calculations were carried out for arbitrary parameters of laser beam polarization, arbitrary angular momenta of optical dipole transitions in the atom and various relaxation processes. The constructed theory allows us to correctly take into account the simultaneous action of various nonlinear optical effects, in contrast to the simplified three-level model of the atom, and also to investigate the partial contribution of each of them to the overall observed resonant circuit. The analysis performed, in particular, made it possible to predict an increase in the amplitude of the sub-Doppler resonance when using counterpropagating light beams with opposite circular polarizations in relation to the configuration of counterpropagating beams with orthogonal linear polarizations, which is often used in practice.

Abstract: In this work, we investigated how variations in spatial confinement affect the electron correlation between two electrons in a symmetric double quantum dot. In strong confinement regime, the ground-state energy can be obtained using the Hartree-Fock method neglecting the electron-electron repulsion term. Under a weaker confinement, the FCI method provides a more accurate description. Using the mutual information as a measure of correlation, we determine the confinement regions where electronic motion is uncorrelated. We conjecture that the Shannon entropy can serve as a qualitative guide to to study the electron correlation. We identified an approximately linear relationship between mutual information and relative correlation energy.

*Daniele Peri*

TL;DR: SIREN 是一种基于 Dawson 方法传统的 Rankine 源面元求解器，用于船舶稳态兴波阻力计算；它用迭代线性化的实际边界 Neumann 问题基流取代经典双体流，实现自由面与船体几何的非线性更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Dawson 方法采用双体流作为基流，其关于 z=0 的镜像仅对平自由面和 z=0 处截断的船体有意义，且需要去奇异的自由面面元。本文旨在消除这些限制。

Method: 自由面条件围绕基流线性化并迭代；基流为实际边界上的 Neumann 问题解——在全部船体面元（湿与干）及自由面面元上布源，并对二者施加不可穿透条件，使自由面充当刚盖。非线性迭代中将自由面面元竖直刚移至其形心处的松弛波高，按移动后的表面将船体面元标记为湿或干，并在移动几何上重算基流；不可穿透条件仍施加于整个船体，标记仅用于选择进入力积分的面元，欠松弛仅限非线性修正累积且未被重置的迭代步。辐射条件通过配点上游偏移一行实现；兴波阻力由压力积分和横向波切分析两种独立途径获得。

Result: 兴波阻力通过压力积分与横向波切分析两种独立方法求得，可相互验证。

Conclusion: 该公式化方法避免了双体流的镜像限制和对去奇异自由面面元的需求，通过实际边界 Neumann 基流与几何非线性迭代，为船舶稳态兴波阻力提供了一种自洽的 Rankine 源面元求解框架。

Abstract: SIREN (Ship Inviscid Resistance and Elevation eNgine) is a Rankine-source panel solver for the steady wave resistance of a ship, in the tradition of Dawson's method. The free-surface condition is linearised about a base flow, and the linearisation is iterated. In the present formulation the base flow is the solution of a Neumann problem on the actual boundary: sources on the entire hull, wet and dry, and on the free surface panels, with impermeability imposed on both, so that the free surface acts as a rigid lid at the position it currently occupies. This replaces the classical double-body flow, whose image about z=0 is meaningful only for a flat surface and a hull cut at z=0, and it removes the need for desingularised free-surface panels. In the non-linear iteration the free-surface panels are moved rigidly in the vertical to the relaxed wave elevation at their centroid, the hull panels are marked wet or dry against the moved surface, and the base flow is recomputed on the moved geometry; impermeability applies to the whole hull regardless of this marking, which is used only to select which panels enter the force integral, and under-relaxation is confined to the passes where the non-linear correction accumulates without being reset. The radiation condition is imposed by a one-row upstream shift of the collocation points, and the wave resistance is obtained by pressure integration and, independently, by transverse wave-cut analysis.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建(1+1)维SU(2)规范耦合Fokker--Planck系统的数值求解器；采用算子分裂格式结合非振荡对流求解器；在弱到强耦合区间（线性与二次耦合，g <= 1.0）进行验证。

Result: 算法严格满足概率守恒、非负性与数值稳定性；非阿贝尔规范耦合表现为交叉反应反馈回路，显著改变系统动力学：驱动状态间持续概率交换、抑制局域密度峰、迫使长时间空间退局域化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了整合复杂多态物理所需的基础计算设施，是改进大气与气候模式中冰云表征的关键一步；同时其底层物理框架与计算引擎具有普遍性和跨学科性，可为任何需要耦合多态随机方程的领域提供通用范式。

Abstract: Extending the Fokker--Planck equation to a coupled system of equations through non-Abelian symmetry groups offers a powerful theoretical framework for coupled multi-state stochastic systems. However, translating this abstract gauge field formulation into viable numerical predictions remains an unaddressed challenge. Motivated by our previous work on ice crystal habit evolution, in this work we bridge this gap by presenting the first numerical solver for an SU(2) gauge-coupled Fokker--Planck system in (1+1) dimensions. Using an operator-splitting scheme combined with a non-oscillatory advection solver, our algorithm strictly ensures probability conservation, non-negativity, and numerical stability. We demonstrate that non-Abelian gauge coupling acts as a cross-reactive feedback loop that fundamentally alters system dynamics, driving continuous probability exchange between states, suppressing localised density peaks, and forcing long-time spatial delocalisation. Validated across linear and quadratic coupling regimes up to strong interactions (g <= 1.0), this work establishes the foundational computational infrastructure needed to integrate complex multi-state physics. Overall, while our approach provides a key step toward improving ice cloud representation in atmospheric and climate models, the underlying physical framework and computational engine remain fundamentally general and multidisciplinary, offering a versatile paradigm for any domain requiring coupled multi-state stochastic equations.

</details>

*Chuanchao Xu,Jun Liu,Dieter Bothe,Tomislav Maric*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: acousticHelmholtzFoam 是一个基于 OpenFOAM 的求解器，用于在非结构多面体网格上计算气液异质介质中的时谐声压场，支持 PML 截断、PETSc 分布式组装与 MUMPS 直接求解，并输出声辐射压力与动量通量等力评估量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气液两相介质中的时谐声场（如声悬浮）需要在复杂几何与异质材料下求解，现有工具缺乏将 VOF 材料建模、PML 开放边界截断、并行直接求解与声力输出一体化的开源实现。

Method: 复声压以实部/虚部两套体积场存储；材料系数由 VOF 相分数经几何面分数平均构造；开放边界用 Cartesian PML 截断，阻尼张量由 setPMLFields 工具生成。两个耦合实值方程采用 OpenFOAM 的单元中心有限体积算子离散，显式组装（含处理器耦合与边界贡献）为分布式 PETSc 矩阵，默认用 MUMPS 稀疏直接求解器求解。

Conclusion: 该文完整记录了方程、算例接口、组装与求解算法、构建流程及测试基础设施，验证了实现的正确性与并行一致性，并与姊妹论文（arXiv:2610.01593）中的数值格式与验证互为补充。

Abstract: acousticHelmholtzFoam is an OpenFOAM application that computes time-harmonic acoustic pressure fields in heterogeneous gas-liquid media on unstructured polyhedral meshes. The complex pressure is stored as real and imaginary volume fields, the material coefficients are constructed from a volume-of-fluid phase fraction with geometric face-fraction averaging, and open boundaries are truncated by Cartesian perfectly matched layers (PMLs) whose damping tensor is generated by the companion utility setPMLFields. The two coupled real-valued equations are discretized with the cell-centred finite-volume operators of OpenFOAM and assembled explicitly, including processor couplings and boundary contributions, into a distributed PETSc matrix that is solved by default with the MUMPS sparse direct solver. The solver writes pressure amplitude, complex velocity, acoustic radiation pressure and time-averaged momentum flux for acoustic-force evaluation. This paper documents the implemented equations, the case interface, the assembly and solution algorithm, the build procedure and the test infrastructure. A layered gas-liquid-PML case with an analytical solution serves as an end-to-end regression: on 8000 cells the whole-domain relative complex-pressure error is $1.197\times10^{-3}$, and serial and MPI solutions on up to eight ranks agree to within $10^{-12}$ in relative $L_2$ norm. A single-axis acoustic-levitator height sweep with constant-volume spheroidal drops demonstrates the two-phase material input and the force output, and strong- and problem-size-scaling measurements characterize the direct-solver workflow. The numerical formulation and its verification are presented in a companion paper (arXiv:2610.01593).

Abstract: The sudden growth in AI data centers is raising numerous environmental concerns. Low-frequency noise pollution is a concern for local residents, that may not just be an annoyance but also carry significant health impacts. An FDTD model of sound propagation over the topography near an AI data center is presented. The topography plays a large role in the level of noise pollution, with areas of sudden elevation on either side of the river near the AI data center more susceptible to high noise levels, while areas further afield largely protected from noise pollution by the topography. Understanding the role that topography plays in noise pollution, and capturing the noise pollution in specific topographies, can help identify areas where noise mitigation strategies might be best employed.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无网格 LBM 在散乱节点集上的半拉格朗日流步是一种插值，本质上是非酉的；对该矩阵进行通用块编码需要与其稀疏度成正比的归一化因子以及对数数量的辅助比特。为克服非酉性并降低量子实现成本，作者寻求一种使流步精确酉化的构造。

Method: 赋予每个节点 Voronoi 质量，并通过质量加权相似变换，结合矩约束与低波数三角差分条件，使模拟导数算子成为斜 Hermite 算子，从而将插值表达为 exp(a Dx + b Dy) 并实现精确酉化。该酉化仅使局部碰撞步在二次项上发生缩放，同一组权重同时用于分布函数的振幅编码。对于准均匀节点集，利用质量加权对角矩阵处于单位阶归一化范围内。方法进一步与 Carleman 嵌入相结合。

Result: 在 Taylor Green 涡数值实验中，该方法能够复现完整的无网格 LBM，且速度误差随 Carleman 阶数增大而降低；质量加权对角仅随 Carleman 截断阶数增长而温和退化，从而缓解块编码的开销。

Conclusion: 通过质量加权的酉化构造，无网格 LBM 的流步可被精确酉化并适配 Carleman 嵌入，在保持与经典无网格 LBM 一致性的同时显著减轻量子块编码的归一化负担，为无网格 LBM 的量子算法实现提供可行路径。

Abstract: We present a mass-weighted meshless formulation for the shifted incompressible lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) whose streaming step is exactly unitary, combining with the Carleman embedding. In traditional meshless LBM, on a scattered node set the semi-Lagrangian streaming step is an interpolation, which is not unitary; a generic block encoding of this matrix therefore requires a normalization proportional to its sparsity and logarithmically many ancillas. To overcome the non-unitarity, we instead express the interpolation as the exponential exp(a Dx + b Dy) of mimetic derivative operators. Endowing each node with its Voronoi mass and applying a mass-weighted similarity transform renders these derivative operators skew-Hermitian by enforcing moment constraints together with low-wave number trigonometric differentiation conditions, so that the interpolation becomes exactly unitary. Such unitarization of interpolation leave the local collision step unchanged up to a rescaling of its quadratic term, where the same weights define the amplitude encoding of the distribution functions. For quasi-uniform node sets, the mass-weighting diagonal remains within a unit-order normalization that degrades only mildly as the Carleman truncation order grows, thereby mitigating the cost of block encoding. Numerical experiments on the Taylor Green vortex confirm that the method reproduces the full meshless LBM, with the velocity error decreasing as the Carleman order increases.

</details>


### [25] [Self-Adaptive Model-Order Reduction for Shock-Dominated Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15944)
*Ali Mohaghegh,Cheng Huang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应模型降阶(MOR)框架，可在在线仿真过程中根据解的演化特征动态选择并部署自适应或静态ROM，从而兼顾计算效率与预测精度，并在旋转爆震发动机(RDE)建模中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态ROM采用离线/在线训练策略，对强对流主导问题（如激波等快速演化物理）预测能力有限；而自适应ROM虽能在线更新低维子空间以跟踪局部演化动力学，却引入额外计算开销，频繁的基与采样点更新会削弱其相对静态ROM的效率优势。因此需要一种能够协调二者权衡的方案。

Method: 提出自自适应MOR框架：在在线模型执行过程中，依据解的演化特征实时动态构建并部署最合适的ROM表述（自适应或静态），以在保证局部物理高建模精度的同时实现最优计算效率。该框架力图同时利用自适应ROM与静态ROM的优势，并减少对大规模离线训练的依赖。

Result: 该框架在旋转爆震发动机(RDE)建模上进行了开发与评估；RDE涉及强对流主导、瞬态、多尺度反应流动力学，构成具有挑战性的测试环境。

Conclusion: 所提自自适应MOR框架通过在线动态切换自适应与静态ROM，可在维持高预测精度的同时优化计算效率并降低离线训练依赖，适用于强对流主导的复杂流动建模。

Abstract: Projection-based model-order reduction (MOR) offers a promising approach for accelerating expensive full-order model (FOM) by constructing reduced-order models (ROMs) that retain the majority of FOM accuracy while significantly reducing the computational cost. However, conventional static ROMs, often following offline/online training strategy, can exhibit limited predictive capability for convection-dominated problems featuring strongly evolving physics such as shocks. Adaptive ROMs emerge as a promising alternative and address such limitation by dynamically updating the low-dimensional subspace during online simulation to track locally evolving system dynamics, thereby enabling accurate predictions even for shock-dominated flows. However, such online adaptation introduces additional computational overhead, and frequent basis and sampling-point updates can substantially reduce their computational advantage relative to their static counterpart. To reconcile this tradeoff, the current work introduces a self-adaptive MOR framework that dynamically constructs and deploys the most appropriate ROM formulation (either adaptive or static) on the fly according to the evolving characteristics of the solution. to achieve optimal computational efficiency while maintaining high modeling accuracy of the local physics during the online model execution. In this regard, the proposed framework seeks to exploit the advantages of both adaptive- and static-ROM approaches to achieve an optimal computational efficiency and predictive accuracy while reducing reliance on extensive offline training. The framework is developed and evaluated for modeling rotating detonation engines (RDEs), which provide a challenging test environment involving strongly convection-dominated, transient, and multiscale reacting-flow dynamics.

</details>


### [26] [Principles and Techniques for Agentic Research in Experimental Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17193)
*Niko R. Reed,Declan M. Daly,Andrew J. Beling,Jordan D. Giandrea,Ronald L. Walsworth,John W. Blanchard,Daniel Ang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出面向实验物理的协作型AI智能体十项要求与实施框架，并通过六个案例验证其有效性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在物理研究中日益普及，但缺乏有效使用指南；需明确AI智能体应作为增强而非替代人类研究者专长的协作者。

Method: 基于原则出发提出十项要求，涵盖科学规范、智能体有效使用与领域专业知识；构建框架，包括LLM定制脚手架、通用科学工作流与技能，并采用闭环式迭代协作模式。

Result: 六个实验物理案例（器件设计、硬件控制、神经网络优化、物理模型识别、数据分析）均产出有用成果，包括超越已发表工作的模型、可制造波导、性能提升逾一个数量级的量子控制脉冲、约1 Hz精度的NMR谱仪调试，以及首次从衍射极限磁成像重建人工自旋冰Ising微态的数据拟合管线。

Conclusion: 人本、闭环的协作AI框架可有效增强实验物理研究，产出显著优于既有工作的成果。

Abstract: The use of artificial intelligence (AI) in physics research is increasingly common, yet few guidelines exist for its effective use. Starting with a principles-based approach, we outline a set of ten requirements for collaborative AI agents in experimental physics, spanning scientific norms, effective agent use, and domain expertise. We take a human-centered approach in which agents act as collaborators that amplify rather than replace the expertise of a human researcher (user) who directs the work and is responsible for its results. We then present a framework designed to enforce these requirements, detailing methods for improving off-the-shelf behavior of large language models (LLMs) with custom scaffolding and describing general-purpose scientific workflows and skills. In particular, we highlight the power of closed-loop campaigns where agents work iteratively toward a well-defined goal. We demonstrate the potential of this mode of research through six case studies in experimental physics, covering device design, hardware control, neural network optimization, physical model identification, and data analysis. All six case studies result in useful work products that add utility to our research, including a neural network architecture and solid-state quantum defect photodynamic model that substantially outperform our published work; a microwave waveguide design that extends a published design concept to a fabrication-ready device meeting our experimental constraints; a quantum control pulse sequence candidate that improves simulated performance by more than an order of magnitude; commissioning of an NMR spectrometer to $\sim$1~Hz frequency precision on live hardware; and finally a data-fitting pipeline that achieves, to our knowledge, the first reconstruction of the Ising microstate of an artificial spin-ice array from diffraction-limited magnetic imaging.

</details>


### [27] [Wisevolve Potential Engine: Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics at 100 Million Atom-Steps per Second on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17366)
*Hekai Bu,Zheyong Fan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出WPE（Wisevolve Potential Engine），利用GPUMD中模型与模拟阶段固定的信息，选择并复用针对性的GPU执行策略，在106个NEP工作负载上实现中位吞吐1.09亿原子步/秒，几何平均加速3.49倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用分子动力学软件需支持多样势函数与模拟需求，但模拟阶段内所加载的势模型与所需计算通常固定不变，这种固定性尚未被用于加速GPU势函数评估。

Method: WPE从GPUMD获取模型与模拟阶段信息，据此选择并复用定制化的GPU执行策略；GPUMD保留模拟控制权与数据所有权，即以非侵入方式将专用化执行策略嵌入通用MD流程。

Result: 单张NVIDIA RTX 5090上，GPUMD-WPE在106个NEP工作负载上中位吞吐1.09亿原子步/秒；1510万原子金刚石Si体系达1.42亿原子步/秒。相对GPUMD几何平均加速：NEP 3.49倍、qNEP 3.61倍、经典势1.24倍、PIMD 10.5倍。水结构、晶格热导、PIMD热膨胀结果与GPUMD及已发表结果一致；1250万原子Cu冲击压缩验证了大规模非平衡动力学适用性。

Conclusion: 模型与阶段专用化是加速通用分子动力学工作流中GPU势函数评估的可行途径。

Abstract: General-purpose molecular dynamics software supports diverse interatomic potentials and simulation requirements. Within a simulation stage, the loaded potential model and the required calculations typically remain fixed as the atomic configuration evolves. We present the Wisevolve Potential Engine (WPE), which uses this information from GPUMD to select and reuse a GPU execution policy tailored to the model and simulation stage. GPUMD retains simulation control and ownership of the simulation data. On a single NVIDIA RTX 5090, WPE-enabled GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) achieves a median throughput of 109 million atom-step/s across 106 neuroevolution potential (NEP) workloads. For a 15.1-million-atom diamond Si system, it sustains 142 million atom-step/s. Relative to GPUMD, geometric-mean speedups are 3.49x across these NEP workloads, 3.61x across 16 workloads using NEP with dynamic charges (qNEP), 1.24x across 28 classical-potential workloads, and 10.5x across 56 path-integral molecular dynamics (PIMD) configurations. End-to-end calculations of water structure, lattice thermal conductivity, and PIMD thermal expansion show close agreement with corresponding GPUMD results and published simulation results. A separate 12.5-million-atom Cu shock-compression simulation demonstrates its applicability to large-scale nonequilibrium dynamics. These results support model- and stage-specific specialization as a practical way to accelerate GPU potential evaluation in general-purpose molecular dynamics workflows.

</details>


### [28] [KINTORA: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin drift-kinetic, unstructured mesh tokamak solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17428)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非结构三角网格、通量坐标无关（FCI）的连续介质漂移动理学求解器，避免场向对齐网格与粒子噪声，全部算子经验证并可在CPU/GPU上复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续介质回旋动理学码依赖嵌套磁面上的结构化场向网格，粒子网格码虽支持非结构网格与开放磁力线却存在统计噪声下限。作者希望发展一种兼具两者优势、既无场向对齐约束又无粒子噪声的求解方案。

Method: 采用半拉格朗日间断伽辽金（SLDG）离散：极向平面为二维非结构三角网格，沿环向拉伸成三维；平行方向推进沿精确磁力线以FCI方式映射，网格始终不与磁场对齐；包含绝热电子与长波极化场求解。

Result: 每个算子均与独立参考解对比验证：从朗道阻尼、测地声学根到线性离子温度梯度本征值问题，除测地声学阻尼误差12%外，其余测量速率误差均在4%以内。在正则麦克斯韦平衡上的线性环向ITG演示得到径向相干模，增长率0.065 c_s/a且随时间稳定。无碰撞带状流残余尚未收敛，随极向加密从模型自身轨道求积预测的0.72升至0.87。求解器完全驻留设备，CPU与GPU构建及不同MPI进程数下结果可复现。

Conclusion: 该SLDG-FCI连续求解器在非结构网格上实现了无噪声、无需场向对齐的漂移动理学模拟，算子验证与线性基准表现良好，跨平台可复现，代码与数据公开；带状流残余的收敛性仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Continuum gyrokinetic codes use structured, field-aligned grids on nested flux surfaces; particle-in-cell codes support unstructured meshes and open field lines but carry a statistical noise floor. We present a continuum drift-kinetic solver that occupies neither position: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin discretization on unstructured triangular poloidal planes extruded toroidally, whose parallel advance is a map along the exact field line in the flux-coordinate independent manner, so the mesh is never aligned with the field, with adiabatic electrons and a long-wavelength polarization field solve. Every operator is verified against an independently computed reference, from Landau damping to the geodesic-acoustic root and a linear ion-temperature-gradient eigenvalue problem, every measured rate within 4% except the geodesic-acoustic damping, within 12%. A linear toroidal ion-temperature-gradient demonstration on a canonical-Maxwellian equilibrium recovers a radially coherent mode whose growth rate, 0.065 c_s/a, is stationary in time. The collisionless zonal-flow residual is the one measurement still moving: it rises with poloidal refinement from 0.72 to 0.87 of the model's own orbit-quadrature prediction for the measured observable and has not converged. The solver is fully device resident, and every result is reproduced between CPU and GPU builds and across MPI process counts. The code, the data behind every table and an artifact description are in a public repository.

</details>

### [29] [A New Degree of Freedom in Stellarator Coil Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14396)
*Sophia A. Henneberg,Annika Zettl*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 反环向场（RTF）线圈为仿星器线圈设计引入新自由度：在总等离子体链环电流由安培定律固定的前提下，通过补偿性RTF电流使模块线圈电流可超过该值而不改变净链环，从而解耦线圈约束、调整外部线圈几何，并在所研究配置中增大线圈-等离子体间距，有望实现更紧凑的仿星器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器线圈设计受多重几何与物理约束限制，线圈与等离子体的间距往往偏小，制约了紧凑化设计。作者希望寻找一种不显著牺牲约束磁场的额外自由度，以解耦关键线圈约束并改善外部线圈几何。

Method: 引入反环向场（RTF）线圈。基于安培定律，总等离子体链环电流固定；通过加入补偿性RTF电流，使模块线圈电流超过该固定值而保持净链环不变，从而重新分配电流并调整外部线圈几何。在多组配置中评估其对约束磁场与线圈-等离子体间距的影响。

Result: 在所研究的配置中，可实现更大的线圈-等离子体间距，同时对约束磁场的削弱不显著，表明RTF电流为线圈几何提供了额外调优空间。

Conclusion: RTF线圈提供了一种新的设计自由度，能够解耦线圈约束并增大线圈-等离子体间距，为实现更紧凑的仿星器设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Reversed toroidal field (RTF) coils introduce a new degree of freedom in stellarator coil design. While the total plasma-linked current is fixed by Ampere's law, compensating RTF currents allow the modular-coil current to exceed this value without altering the net linkage. This redistribution decouples key coil constraints and modifies the external coil geometry without significantly compromising the confining magnetic field. As a result, the achievable coil-plasma separation can be increased in the configurations studied, potentially enabling more compact stellarator designs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强场QED条件下激光-等离子体相互作用需要同时自洽描述经典等离子体动力学、非线性康普顿散射/布里特-惠勒对产生等随机量子过程，以及电子自旋和光子偏振的自由度；现有代码缺乏将这些模块统一耦合的实现。

Result: 在1、2、3维基准中验证实现；经典PIC结果与SMILEI交叉比对；自旋与偏振分辨QED模块复现了极化电子产生、真空双折射与二向色性、以及稠密极化正电子产生的已发表结果。

Conclusion: 这些基准验证了SPIRE中耦合的经典PIC、强场QED、自旋和光子偏振模块的正确性与自洽性。

Abstract: We present SPIRE, a spin- and photon-polarization-resolved particle-in-cell (PIC) code for self-consistent simulations of laser-plasma interactions in the strong-field quantum electrodynamics (QED) regime. The classical PIC framework combines a Yee-grid finite-difference time-domain Maxwell solver, a relativistic Boris particle pusher, shape-function particle-grid interpolation, Esirkepov charge-conserving current deposition, laser boundary injection, and a relativistic moving window in one, two, and three spatial dimensions. A vectorial Laguerre-Gaussian laser injector based on an angular-spectrum formulation supports tightly focused, nonparaxial optical orbital-angular-momentum modes. The strong-field QED module treats nonlinear Compton scattering and nonlinear Breit-Wheeler pair production as stochastic processes using spin- and polarization-resolved probabilities in the local constant-field approximation. Electron and positron spin evolution includes stochastic transitions, no-emission corrections, and Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi precession with a field-dependent anomalous magnetic moment. Photon polarization is represented by the Stokes vector and evolves through nonlinear Compton scattering, pair production, vacuum birefringence, and vacuum dichroism. The implementation is verified with one-, two-, and three-dimensional benchmarks. Classical PIC results are cross-checked against SMILEI, and the spin- and polarization-resolved QED modules reproduce published results for polarized electron generation, vacuum birefringence and dichroism, and dense polarized positron production. These benchmarks verify the coupled classical PIC, strong-field QED, spin, and photon-polarization modules implemented in SPIRE.

</details>

Abstract: Here,we report an ST plasma in which Thomson scattering measures a central electron temperature exceeding 8.6 keV (100 million degrees kelvin) on the EXL-50U spherical torus device.The high-temperature phase is obtained at low density by sequential operation of two 50 GHz ECRH systems: ordinary-mode (O-mode) ECRH3 followed by extraordinary-mode (X-mode) ECRH4. In contrast to neutral-beam-heated high-ion-temperature ST scenarios, the discharge is sustained by ECRH only.

Method: 针对氢等离子体与氢中性原子相互作用，在动理学与回旋动理学框架下推导等离子体-中性粒子相互作用项；考虑速度依赖的微分截面，以捕捉非弹性碰撞对离子和电子的动理学影响；并在适当的麦克斯韦性与线性假设下，验证动理学模型的流体矩可退化为标准流体源项；最后通过分布函数与流体矩对比动理学模型与流体模型。

Abstract: Plasma-neutral interactions critically affect the edge dynamics of magnetically confined fusion devices. Neutral atoms modify ion and electron velocity distributions, potentially affecting local instabilities Standard fluid models simplify these processes (namely charge exchange, ionization and recombination) using Maxwellian assumptions and linearized source terms. We revisit these assumptions for a hydrogen plasma interacting with hydrogen atoms. We derive plasma-neutral interaction terms in the kinetic and gyrokinetic frameworks. Accounting for velocity-dependent differential cross-sections, the model captures the kinetic impact of inelastic collisions on ions and electrons beyond energy and momentum exchange. We demonstrate that the fluid moments of the kinetic model reduce to standard fluid source terms under the appropriate assumptions of Maxwellianity and linearity. We compare the kinetic model with fluid models through their effects on distribution functions and fluid moments. Substantial discrepancies are observed, even for Maxwellian distributions. For charge exchange, the kinetic model yields momentum and energy exchange rates up to twice those predicted by fluid descriptions. We show that a fluid treatment of ionization greatly overestimates the mean kinetic energy of the produced electrons. These results highlight the limitations of fluid descriptions and the importance of kinetic effects in edge-plasma momentum and energy exchange.

</details>

Abstract: A unified framework is proposed to compute a neoclassical equilibrium for tokamaks with non-axisymmetric magnetic perturbations, based on a Hamiltonian description combined with a variational approach that relies on a principle of minimum entropy production. This approach allows for a transparent identification of the respective contributions from different classes of trapped particles in magnetic well minima. Analytic expressions are derived for specific collisionality regimes, and a fast and efficient numerical procedure is devised to compute the entropy production rate. Finding the extremum of the entropy production leads to tractable expressions of transport coefficients that can be computed quickly and efficiently.

Abstract: We present a quasi-static particle-in-cell scheme for beam-driven plasma wakefields in cylindrical geometry that works on an arbitrary non-uniform radial grid. An optional azimuthal mode $m=1$ describes beam offsets, tilts and hosing. The discretization keeps the discrete Gauss law exact, deposits a uniform plasma exactly on every node including the axis, and remains second-order accurate on smoothly stretched grids. Resolution can therefore be placed where the physics needs it. For a tightly focused witness beam that pulls the plasma ions onto the axis, a grid that is fine only near the axis gives the same near-axis fields as a uniform grid with the same axis resolution, with thirty times fewer cells and in thirty times less computing time. Such a grid also resolves the singular closure of a cold-plasma bubble, which a uniform grid hides by smoothing it over one cell; smoothing the plasma sources over a fixed length, chosen independently of the grid, regularizes the closure and makes the wake behind it converge. The scheme is implemented in the open-source C++/Kokkos code QUARZ (Quasistatic Arbitrary-resolution RZ), which runs on CPUs and GPUs and uses MPI with a pipelined decomposition along the co-moving coordinate. Simulations of the self-modulation of the AWAKE proton bunch agree with the three-dimensional code HiPACE++. QUARZ is, to our knowledge, the first production-grade simulation code in plasma physics that is \emph{zero-touch}: all of its source code, tests and documentation were written by an AI agent directed by a physicist who did not write a single line of the code.

Abstract: A dynamic optimization workflow for linear gyrokinetic stability that couples the gyrokinetic code CGYRO with the libEnsemble framework for ensemble management has been developed. Rather than optimizing the growth rate at a single wavenumber, the objective maximizes the growth rate-to-wavenumber ratio over the ion-scale spectrum. A derivative-free, model-based optimization algorithm employs manifold sampling to handle the nonsmooth composite structure of the objective function. The approach is applied to the optimization of local magnetic flux-surface shaping parameters, with an equilibrium baseline drawn from DIII-D negative triangularity experimental parameters. Abstract: Negative Triangularity (NT) tokamak plasmas can achieve H-mode-like confinement while avoiding Edge Localized Modes, making them an attractive reactor scenario. This prediction is confirmed by linear and nonlinear GENE simulations in both ITG- and TEM-dominated regimes. Additionally, a dedicated experimental elongation scan on TCV shows that NT plasmas at $κ^{\rm LCFS}=1.5$ have $β_N$ and $H_{98y2}$ roughly 1.5 times larger than PT counterparts, while no difference is observed at $κ^{\rm LCFS}=0.95$. The analysis is then extended to realistic ITG-dominated scenarios from DIII-D, AUG, and JET, including up-down asymmetric equilibria where conventional triangularity-based descriptions break down. In all three devices, simulations of NT equilibria exhibit substantially reduced nonlinear heat fluxes relative to PT (factors of 1.8, 1.6, and 2.1, respectively). A systematic swap of geometric coefficients between NT and PT equilibria shows that magnetic drifts and FLR effects are the dominant stabilizing mechanisms across all machines, with parallel dynamics playing a secondary role. These results establish the geometric proxies as reliable, computationally inexpensive predictors of NT transport benefits across diverse shapes and turbulence regimes.

Abstract: Finite extinction ratio in pulse-modulated Brillouin optical time-domain reflectometry (BOTDR) produces residual inter-pulse Brillouin backscatter outside the nominal pulse gate. Measurements were performed using a 1.2 km standard single-mode fiber (SMF) followed by 0.1 km of dispersion-compensating fiber (DCF), whose separated Brillouin frequency shifts allow section-to-section leakage to be resolved spectrally. At 20 m spatial resolution, increasing the extinction ratio from 18.5 to 33.0 dB reduced the SMF-band contamination in the DCF gates from -5.3 to -14.8 dB. Over the 16-33 dB range, the SCR showed fitted slopes of -1.064 and -1.246 dB/dB for the two contamination directions. These results quantify finite-ER-induced spatial nonlocality and provide a link-specific metric for extinction-ratio selection in BOTDR.

Together, these results establish adaptive multifocal illumination as a practical route to high-throughput aberration-free fluorescence response matrix imaging.

Abstract: Light reflected from a deforming solid depends on boundary motion and strain-induced modulation of the dielectric response. For a propagating acoustic field, scattering from different depths interferes coherently, so the measured optical amplitude and phase depend jointly on the strain profile, dielectric constant, and photoelastic constant. Optical tomography often makes such reconstructions tractable through independent projections. To probe mechanical strain, however, the optical measurement also depends on the material properties that mediate its observation, requiring those properties to be known or assumptions about the field. Here we show that broadband picosecond ultrasonic echoes in an opaque solid can supply the missing information. Fourier transformation of the measured complex relative reflectance change obtained with monochromatic light at normal incidence gives access to many acoustic frequency components. We recover the complex dielectric and photoelastic constants together with the nanoscale depth-dependent strain profile by formulating the reconstruction as a multiparameter nonlinear eigenvalue problem. We demonstrate the method using experimental echoes from a chromium film. The recovered refractive index agrees with ellipsometry, whereas the recovered strain profile closely follows a two-temperature-model prediction including electron and thermal diffusion and frequency-dependent ultrasonic attenuation. Synthetic echoes test robustness to added noise. The evaluation of the refractive index is necessary to design photonic devices for various classical and quantum photonic applications, and to theoretically analyse experimental results. Although the refractive index can be measured experimentally, its estimation via computational means provide the advantage of not requiring costly instrumentation, trained researchers and could avoid potential laboratory-based uncertainties. The implementation of transfer learning allows training the neural networks for a new material with higher speed or accuracy when compared to non-knowledge transfer-based training. Our results show the efficiency of the proposed technique in accurately evaluating the real and imaginary parts of the refractive index of different materials.

The resulting spatiotemporally partially coherent wave packets exhibit non-separable space-time intensity profiles together with engineered spatiotemporal correlation distributions. Abstract: Phase retrieval recovers complex-valued objects from intensity-only measurements. In its classical Fourier form, reconstruction suffers from twin-image stagnation. Coded diffraction imaging, which adds a random phase mask before the Fourier transform, has so far required several masks. We show that an image prior makes single-shot coded diffraction imaging possible, with a single random mask followed by a Fourier transform. We validate this claim in simulation and in an optical experiment, in which a spatial light modulator displays the phase object together with the random mask. Our experimental validation covers five test signals and 530 images from the public Brno quantitative phase microscopy dataset of five adherent cell lines. With no image prior or with Fourier-only measurements, reconstruction of complex-valued objects from a single diffraction pattern consistently fails. An out-of-the-box pretrained image denoiser enables accurate reconstruction, in simulation even with fewer measurements than unknowns. Five regularization frameworks built on the same denoiser and one learned weakly convex regularizer all reconstruct the experimental images with peak signal-to-noise ratios within one decibel of each other. A single random phase mask and image priors thus turn coded diffraction imaging into a practical single-shot modality for quantitative phase imaging.

</details>

Abstract: Molecular chiral discrimination is vital across chemistry and life sciences, yet optical approaches remain highly inefficient for small organic molecules. Here we turn vector beams, whose polarization carries a topological charge, into an efficient probe of molecular handedness. We show that the combination of a tightly focused radially polarized beam at the fundamental frequency ω and an azimuthally polarized beam at 2ω creates chiral vector beams, a locally chiral field with structured handedness. Using state-of-the-art computational modeling in the chiral molecule carvone, we show that the nonlinear response (notably sum-frequency generation) of randomly oriented chiral molecules to this chiral vector beam captures the fields' local handedness and is highly enantio-sensitive. The two enantiomers emit light at frequency 3ω with topological ring structures of different divergence, and thus the molecular handedness can be read out directly from the ring radius. The signal inherits the Poincaré index of the driving field, opening up new ways to robust, ultrafast, and and all-optical chiral discrimination.

Abstract: Photons emitted by incoherent sources exhibit temporal bunching, a fundamental manifestation of Bose-Einstein statistics first observed by Hanbury Brown and Twiss (HBT). While intensity correlation measurements originated in astronomy and are now central across quantum technologies, biophysics, and spectroscopy, observing temporal bunching in broadband thermal light remains limited by detector temporal resolution. Conventional approaches - such as streak cameras or optical upconversion - introduce severe trade-offs in efficiency, bandwidth, or setup versatility. Here, we propose and demonstrate a third route: pre-detection temporal magnification of the optical field prior to measuring its intensity autocorrelation function in a conventional HBT setup. Using an electro-optic time lens with a temporal magnification of 660, we resolve the temporal intensity autocorrelation of broadband thermal light generated via non-degenerate spontaneous parametric down-conversion with a bandwidth of 250 GHz. This enables direct observation of thermal photon bunching on sub-3.5 picosecond timescales without narrow spectral filtering, establishing pre-detection temporal imaging as a general platform for broadband statistical optics, with applications spanning ultrafast quantum optics, biophysical imaging, and potentially stellar intensity interferometry.

</details>

Abstract: Optical generation of terahertz (0.1 to 10\,THz) carriers can, in principle, preserve the spectral purity of an optical reference without the noise penalty associated with electronic frequency multiplication. Here, we identify and suppress the dominant excess-noise mechanisms that limit this conversion in high-power uni-travelling-carrier photodiodes. We characterize amplitude-to-phase conversion as functions of photocurrent and reverse bias and identify operating parameters to minimize the effect. We quantify the white phase noise introduced by optical amplification and show that this contribution can be eliminated by directly driving the photodiode with a high-power optical source. With the excess noise mitigated, we use cross-correlation phase-noise measurements to observe a white phase-noise floor of $-161.0$~dBc/Hz at 263.2~GHz, corresponding to a timing-noise floor of 7.6~zs/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. These results establish direct photomixing as a route to sub-10-zs/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ white timing-noise floors for continuous-wave sources at terahertz frequencies and identify the noise processes that must be controlled to reach this regime.

Abstract: Accurate lens surface metrology is essential to determine the manufactured geometry of refractive optical elements and their deviation from the specified design. Abstract: We develop a physically interpretable inverse-design framework for generalized Huygens metasurfaces, using prescribed scattering poles and zeros to engineer resonance interference and transmission-phase control. The resulting designs demonstrate how near-unity transmission and $2π$ phase excursion can arise from resonance interference. These signatures of the Kerker effect extend beyond the conventional alignment of electric and magnetic dipole resonances to other mechanisms, including guided-mode resonances and quasi-bound states in the continuum. We further optimize the Kerker condition across a continuum of refractive-index states in a reconfigurable phase change material metasurface, enabling high-transmission $2π$ phase tuning. Our results establish a versatile and practical approach to metasurface phase control through the targeted design of scattering poles and zeros.

Abstract: We develop a theory of ionization-assisted instability that governs the reflection of intense electromagnetic pulses from a semiconductor surface. Local overheating of the material increases the free-carrier density and conductivity, which in turn enhances the absorption of the incident radiation and further amplifies local heating and avalanche ionization. At high pump repetition rates, the overheating accumulates from pulse to pulse, leading to a gradual rise of the local material temperature and free carrier density - a positive feedback loop that drives thermal runaway. However, this effect competes with diffraction of the pump field on the emerging perturbation of the dielectric permittivity or on the surface relief, which reduces the local electric field amplitude in the overheated regions, thus providing negative feedback. We show that the competition between heat diffusion and diffraction near-field weakening creates conditions for the exponential growth of temperature perturbations with a certain wavenumber determined by the pulse repetition rate, pump field, thermal conductivity and other material parameters. The derived analytical expressions accurately describe the spatial period of deeply subwavelength structures observed in recent experiments on silicon structuring by intense terahertz radiation, and reproduce qualitatively dependencies of their period on the pump intensity, the incident wavelength and the number of pump pulses.

Abstract: Optical isolation suppresses back-reflections that destabilize lasers, produce parasitic interference, and increase crosstalk in photonic systems. Although free-space and fibre-optic isolators are mature, no broadly adopted on-chip technology yet combines strong isolation, low insertion loss, broad bandwidth, compact footprint, and scalable fabrication. The difficulty is twofold: Lorentz reciprocity must be broken, and every relevant backward spatial, spectral, and polarization channel must be suppressed. This Review develops a common framework for assessing proposed solutions. We first distinguish pairwise nonreciprocity from intensity-asymmetric transmission and from true isolation using the constitutive relations and scattering matrix. We then compare three physical routes to an integrated nonreciprocal response: magneto-optical bias, externally driven space-time modulation, and nonlinear optical processes. Topological and non-Hermitian systems are treated as cross-cutting design frameworks that can route, protect, or enhance an underlying nonreciprocal response, but do not by themselves break reciprocity. Finally, we examine fundamental constraints and practical trade-offs among isolation, insertion loss, bandwidth, footprint, modal coverage, drive requirements, and fabrication compatibility. The resulting mechanism-based perspective connects reciprocity-breaking physics to the requirements of scalable photonic integrated circuits.

Abstract: Traditional optical system design for imaging applications has long been guided by image quality metrics such as geometrical RMS spot size, wavefront error, and modulation transfer function (MTF). Although these metrics effectively characterize direct imaging performance, they do not necessarily indicate optimal behavior when image restoration is subsequently applied. In modern imaging pipelines, however, optical systems are increasingly combined with physics-based or learning-based restoration methods, making the information transfer properties of the optical system critically important. In this work, we propose a recovery-oriented optical design approach that explicitly links optical system optimization with image restoration performance through an estimation-theoretic framework based on Fisher information. A new design criterion is adopted to ensure that the information encoded by the optical system can be reliably recovered during image restoration. To validate the proposed approach, two dual separated aspheric lenses are designed under identical configurations using conventional RMS-based optimization and the proposed recovery-oriented optimization, respectively. Although the RMS-optimized lens exhibits superior direct imaging quality, it underperforms after image restoration compared to the recovery-oriented design. These results demonstrate that recovery-oriented optical design provides a physically interpretable and effective extension of traditional optical design criteria for imaging systems operating within modern computational imaging pipelines.

Abstract: Organisms have evolved diverse strategies to communicate. Beyond acoustic and visual signals, many species rely on chemical communication, including pheromones: volatile compounds involved in reproduction, defense, and coordination. Ant trail systems, a paradigm of pheromone use, have inspired decades of research in collective behavior, self-organization and optimization algorithms. Yet, despite their central role, individual pheromone deposits have never been directly visualized or quantified, limiting our understanding of how deposition events scale into collective trail formation. Here, we use interferometric imaging to reveal and quantify single pheromone deposits in the ant Lasius niger. This label-free, non-invasive approach enables precise measurement of single deposit volume (~1 pL, with x20-fold variability), morphology, and temporal dynamics. We uncover a two-phase volume loss process: most of secreted volume evaporates within seconds, forming a dried droplet, which then partially sublimates (~10%) over one hour. These findings challenge the assumption of homogeneous deposition and bring previously inaccessible properties of ant pheromone trails within experimental reach. Our method establishes a quantitative framework for studying the spatiotemporal dynamics of insect chemical communication, linking individual signaling to collective patterns.

Abstract: Chiral materials manipulate light in ways that hold promise for sensing, imaging, and photonics, but producing such materials with strong and reproducible optical activity at large scale remains challenging. Abstract: Chip-scale microresonator-based frequency combs, commonly referred to as microcombs, are promising sources for field-deployable metrology applications. Here, we propose an optoelectronic self-trapping scheme in which the impact of intrinsic microresonator noise on microcomb repetition-rate jitter is suppressed through feedback-controlled phase modulation of the driving laser so that the intrinsic noise of the laser sets the ultimate performance limit. This modification eliminates the need for an external low-phase-noise RF source, thereby enabling chip integration. We develop both analytical and numerical models for the proposed system and benchmark its performance against conventional externally referenced bulk implementations, showing that the proposed approach achieves comparable microcomb noise suppression while maintaining chip-scale compatibility.

Abstract: Spatiotemporal optical vortices (STOVs) are structured light featuring a phase singularity in the spatiotemporal domain, exhibiting unique topology that couples spatial and temporal dimensions. In this work, we present the theoretical analysis and experimental demonstration of optical parametric amplification (OPA) of STOVs. The pulse energy of the STOV signal is enhanced by a factor of 60, while its spatiotemporal structure is robustly preserved. The generated idler inherits the phase-conjugated STOV topology with the opposite topological charge, governed by simultaneous conservation of energy, linear momentum, and spatiotemporal orbital angular momentum. The intrinsic wavelength-conversion capability of OPA further provides a route towards spectrally tunable STOV generation, spanning from visible to near-infrared wavelength regimes. This work paves the way toward further development of multi-stage amplification architectures, facilitating applications of wavelength-tunable spatiotemporally structured light at ultrahigh intensities.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想单模近似无法刻画真实电子束的有限横向动量展宽与光栅前积累的Hartree相位，需要更贴近实际的模型来理解叉形光栅生成电子涡旋束的OAM统计与横向纯度。

Method: 将入射束描述为具有有限横向动量展宽的傍轴高斯系综，并引入光栅前累积的一阶电磁Hartree相位；在Fresnel传播框架下，推导各衍射级的解析表达式并计算相应的轨道角动量（OAM）统计；采用径向相位屏近似处理Hartree自场。

Result: 选定衍射级的平均OAM仍由拓扑规则固定，即⟨L_z⟩_m = mpℏ；其方差由光栅处束的残余横向动量展宽决定。对高斯混合，横向纯度为P_⊥,m = [1 + 2(ΔL_{z,m}/ℏ)^2]^{-1}。观测到的环形轮廓由独立的、依赖传播的模糊尺度决定，因此束可以保持良好定义的平均OAM，同时具有低横向纯度或部分填充的涡旋极小值。在径向相位屏近似下，Hartree自场改变传播轮廓，但保持全平面OAM矩和横向纯度不变。

Conclusion: 超越单模近似后，平均OAM的拓扑规则依然稳健，而方差、横向纯度与环形轮廓由束的横向动量展宽和传播效应控制；Hartree自场仅影响传播轮廓，不改变OAM矩与横向纯度。

Abstract: We analyze fork-grating generation of electron vortex beams beyond the ideal single-mode approximation. The incident beam is described as a paraxial Gaussian ensemble with finite transverse momentum spread and a first electromagnetic Hartree phase accumulated before the grating. Within Fresnel propagation we derive an analytical expression for each diffraction order and determine the corresponding orbital-angular-momentum statistics. The mean OAM of a selected order remains fixed by the topological rule, $\langle L_z\rangle_m=mp\hbar$, while its variance is set by the residual transverse momentum spread of the beam prepared at the grating. For the Gaussian mixture, the transverse purity is $\mathcal P_{\perp,m}=[1+2(ΔL_{z,m}/\hbar)^2]^{-1}$. The observed annular profile is governed by a separate propagation-dependent blurring scale, so a beam can retain well-defined mean OAM while having low transverse purity or a partially filled vortex minimum. Within the radial phase-screen approximation, the Hartree self-field modifies the propagated profile while preserving the full-plane OAM moments and transverse purity.

</details>

At this power, oxygen admission leaves a background of 1.22 kHz, corresponding to an estimated signal-to-background ratio of 3.8 x 10^4 after current-integration calibration. In a separate 3-h record acquired with 1-ms gates, local referencing achieves near-Poisson averaging, reaching a fractional Allan deviation of 3.5 x 10^-5 at 1000 s. Conclusion: 上述功率二次依赖关系、环境衰减及偏振特性共同证实了真空紫外的产生，表明BaMgF4是一种具有潜力的连续波真空紫外非线性介质。

These results highlight the potential of BMF as a nonlinear medium for CW VUV generation.

### [86] [Mode locking by phase-sensitive amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18559)
*Francesco De Lucia,Yifan Sun,Georges Semaan,Clement Dupont,Nicolas Englebert,Simon-Pierre Gorza,François Leo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出并实验实现“相位敏感锁模”机制，将脉冲形成与光相位稳定统一到同一非线性反馈中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统锁模机制只选择脉冲振幅，而不锁定脉冲相对于外部光参考的相位，导致脉冲形成与光相位稳定是两个独立功能。作者希望让外部光参考直接参与脉冲形成。

Method: 在连续泵浦振荡器中利用克尔效应引起的相位演化与腔失谐，将参考于泵浦的参量增益转化为功率相关的判别器，从而支持稳定的单孤子运行；并用往返分辨干涉测量表征孤子的光相位。

Result: 实验实现了稳定的单孤子运行；往返分辨干涉测量显示，孤子光相位相对于主激光保持参考关系（模π）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了相位敏感锁模，其中脉冲振幅选择与光相位参考源自同一个相干非线性反馈，为频率梳、精密定时与光谱学等提供新机制。

Abstract: Mode locking is the mechanism by which lasers organize many optical modes into stable trains of ultrashort pulses. It underpins optical frequency combs, precision timing and spectroscopy, and provides the phase-coherent waveforms required for applications ranging from strong-field physics to optical frequency metrology. Conventional pulse-forming mechanisms select pulse amplitude without fixing its phase relative to an external optical reference, leaving pulse formation and optical-phase stabilization as separate functions. Phase-sensitive amplification offers a different possibility: the optical reference can participate directly in pulse formation. Here we demonstrate this principle in a continuously pumped oscillator. Kerr-induced phase evolution and cavity detuning turn pump-referenced parametric gain into a power-dependent discriminator that supports stable single-soliton operation. Roundtrip-resolved interferometry reveals that the soliton optical phase remains referenced to the master laser modulo $π$. These results establish phase-sensitive mode locking, in which pulse-amplitude selection and optical-phase referencing arise from the same coherent nonlinear feedback.

</details>


### [87] [Probing topological edge states in multilayer dielectric photonic nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18700)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑光子学虽已引发大量理论研究与预测，但拓扑驱动效应的直接实验验证，尤其在纳米结构中，仍鲜有证明，因此需要实验确证拓扑边缘态的存在及其物理效应。

Method: 构建由交替亚波长介质层组成、遵循SSH拓扑模型排列的多层光子纳米结构，通过工程化不同边缘截断来探测不同拓扑相，并对表面场分布进行理论预测与实验测量。

Result: 理论与实验测量均显示，仅在支持拓扑边缘态的构型中表面出现强场局域，证明其局域特征源于拓扑性质；在表面沉积超薄吸收层时观察到增强吸收，同时从表面反射中观察到增强的二次谐波产生。

Conclusion: 实验证实了纳米结构中拓扑边缘态的表面场局域及其拓扑起源，展示了其在表面传感与表面增强非线性光学方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Topological photonics has recently attracted a significant attention, leading to many theoretical studies and predictions. However, direct experimental demonstrations of topology-driven effects, particularly in nanostructures, are rarely proven. In this work, we experimentally investigate multilayer photonic nanostructures composed of alternating subwavelength dielectric layers, arranged according to the topological Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. By engineering different edge truncations, we probe different supported topological phases. Our theoretical predictions and experimental measurements reveal strong field confinement at the surface exclusively in the configuration supporting topological edge states, providing their localized character and topological origin. First, we demonstrate enhanced absorption in an ultrathin absorbing overlayer deposited on the surface when the topological surface mode is present, highlighting the potential for surface-sensing applications. Second, we observe enhanced second-harmonic generation in reflection from the surface, demonstrating the potential of topological states for surface enhanced nonlinear optics enabled by the strong localization of the electromagnetic field at the interface.

</details>


### [88] [Engineering Color and Angular Stability in Building-Integrated Photovoltaics Using Metal-Oxide Photonic Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18759)
*Sinan Genc,Talat Ozden,Alpan Bek*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过耦合Mie散射、Percus-Yevick结构因子、精确相函数与含吸收和折射的双通量传输模型，在真实层叠组件上量化了非晶光子玻璃用于建筑一体化光伏的角独立着色性能，识别出蓝、绿、金、陶土四种目标颜色的配方与设计约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑一体化光伏的着色面临两难：吸收型颜料损失光电流，有序光子结构则产生虹彩。非晶光子玻璃（高折射率纳米球的无序堆积）被提出可同时规避这两个问题，但其角度无关颜色很少在真实组件层叠上被量化。此外，非吸收散射体需要高折射率且可见光透明，而Moss规则将其上限限制在约2.35。

Method: 耦合Mie散射、Percus-Yevick结构因子、精确相函数以及包含吸收和前玻璃处折射的双通量传输模型。通过众包命名调查确定四种颜色（蓝、绿、金、陶土），并在四种金属氧化物、四种封装材料、两种电池技术以及广泛的粒径、堆积分数和层数范围内映射达到这些颜色的配方。

Result: 四种颜色均可在三层叠层涂层中实现，DeltaE00为0.8至2.5，但并非来自单一氧化物：透明TiO2只能呈现蓝色和浅暖色调；绿、金、陶土需要V2O5的蓝带吸收；低对比度氧化物在暖端变灰。封装材料主导颜色，电池技术则不影响。无序并不带来角度独立性：在固定太阳下移动观察者时颜色保持，但全天漂移DeltaE00为5至18，陶土最轻微；从上方照明、从下方观察的立面会使所有颜色偏蓝。

Conclusion: 氧化物、层数和立面几何必须协同设计。非晶光子玻璃不能保证角度独立颜色，封装材料是关键变量，而电池技术无关紧要。

Abstract: Coloring building-integrated photovoltaics poses a dilemma: absorptive pigments cost photocurrent, while ordered photonic structures are iridescent. Amorphous photonic glasses, disordered packings of high-index nanospheres, are proposed to avoid both, yet their angle-independent color has rarely been quantified on a realistic module stack. A non-absorbing scatterer needs a high index with visible transparency, which the Moss rule caps near 2.35. Coupling Mie scattering, the Percus-Yevick structure factor, the exact phase function, and two-flux transfer with absorption and refraction at the front glass, we fix four target colors that a crowd-sourced naming survey calls unambiguously blue, green, gold, and terracotta, and map the recipes reaching them across four metal oxides, four encapsulants, two cell technologies, and wide ranges of particle size, packing fraction, and layer number. All four are reached in a laminated three-layer coating, to within DeltaE00 of 0.8 to 2.5, but not from one oxide: transparent TiO2 renders the blue and only pale warm tones; the green, gold, and terracotta require the blue-band absorption of V2O5, and the low-contrast oxides turn grey at the warm end. The encapsulant governs the color, and the cell technology does not. Disorder does not confer angle independence: the colors hold for an observer moving under a fixed sun but drift by DeltaE00 of 5 to 18 over the day, terracotta least, and a facade lit from above and viewed from below shifts every color towards the blue. Oxide, layer number, and facade geometry must therefore be designed together.

</details>
