### [89] [A semi-infinite hydraulic fracture propagating in a poroelastic medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14049)
*Evgenii Kanin,Andreas Möri,Dmitry Garagash,Brice Lecampion*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文求解了可渗透孔隙弹性介质中水力裂缝扩展的近尖端耦合水力-力学问题，通过黏度-韧性标度识别出四个特征性孔隙弹性响应区，并给出了简化模型的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水力压裂的近尖端区域同时涉及流体流动、岩石变形和孔隙流体交换（漏失与渗入），三者相互耦合。已有简化模型（非耦合、单向耦合）忽略了孔隙弹性回应力等效应，其适用性缺乏定量评估，因此需要一个完全耦合的近尖端解析-数值解来厘清耦合效应的相对重要性。

Method: 将问题局部化为以恒定速度移动的半无限裂缝，在移动尖端坐标系中求解稳态场。固体采用线性孔隙弹性理论，裂缝扩展遵循线弹性断裂力学（LEFM），缝内流动由润滑理论描述，压裂液与孔隙流体视为相同牛顿流体。以缝宽和流体位移函数为基本未知量，求解非线性方程组，并采用黏度-韧性标度，得到四个无量纲参数：无量纲渗透率$\varrho$、无量纲有效围压$Σ_0'$、排水-不排水泊松比差$β$以及孔隙弹性应力系数$η$。在代表多种岩石类型的参数范围内进行数值求解，并在实验室至现场尺度上与非耦合和单向耦合模型对比。

Result: 解从排水的韧性主导近尖端渐近解（净压力均匀）过渡到不排水的储存-黏度主导远场渐近解。在$(\varrho,Σ_0')$参数空间中的相对差异图识别出四个特征区：(1) 孔隙弹性效应可忽略区；(2) 直接力学孔隙弹性响应主导区；(3) 孔隙弹性回应力重要区；(4) 完全耦合区。完全耦合主要出现在大$\varrho$、小$Σ_0'$的条件下，即渗入相对于漏失占优的情形。

Conclusion: 孔隙弹性耦合对水力裂缝近尖端场的影响可由$(\varrho,Σ_0')$参数空间划分为四个区，简化模型仅在特定区域内有效；在强渗入、弱漏失条件下必须采用完全耦合模型。

Abstract: We solve the near-tip coupled hydromechanical problem of a hydraulic fracture propagating in a permeable poroelastic medium, represented locally as a semi-infinite crack moving at constant velocity. In the moving tip frame, all fracture fields are steady. The porous solid obeys linear poroelasticity, fracture propagation follows LEFM, fluid flow within the tensile fracture is governed by lubrication theory, and the fracturing and pore fluids are treated as identical Newtonian liquids. The primary unknowns are the fracture opening and the fluid displacement function along the fracture, determined from the resulting non-linear system of equations. In a viscosity--toughness scaling, the problem depends on four dimensionless parameters: a dimensionless permeability $\varrho$ (associated with the intensity of fluid leak-in), a dimensionless effective confining stress $Σ_0'$ (measuring the relative importance of fluid leak-off and leak-in), the drained--undrained Poisson's ratio contrast $β$, and the poroelastic stress coefficient $η$. The solution evolves from a drained, toughness-dominated near-tip asymptote with uniform net pressure to an undrained, storage-viscosity-dominated far-field asymptote. Numerical solutions are obtained over parameter ranges representative of various rock types. The fully coupled model is compared with simplified models, namely uncoupled and one-way coupled formulations, over distances from the fracture front spanning laboratory-to-field scales. The resulting relative difference maps in the $(\varrho,Σ_0')$ parameter space identify four characteristic regimes: negligible poroelastic effects, dominance of the direct mechanical poroelastic response, importance of the poroelastic backstress, and a fully coupled regime. The latter occurs mainly at large $\varrho$ and small $Σ_0'$, corresponding to strong leak-in relative to leak-off.

</details>


### [90] [CFD Forge: A Physics-Constrained Agentic Framework for Autonomous CFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14104)
*Aakash Shailesh Oza,Ryan F. Johnson,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出CFD Forge：以OpenFOAM为核心的智能体框架，将"做工作"（LLM驱动的建模、网格、求解、诊断）与"做判定"（确定性检查）分离，实现可信的自动化CFD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD仿真的建立、运行与评估依赖大量人工操作（网格生成、求解器设置、诊断与解读），在设计变体间重复执行会拖慢工程迭代；而自动化只有在结果可信时才有价值，因此不能让模型自己判断是否接受结果。

Method: LLM解析工程请求，读取仿真证据、识别问题原因并从封闭动作集中提出操作；验收由按流型注册的确定性检查基于显式准则判定，而非模型。在收敛-扩张喷管与二维前向台阶（含文本指定的几何与工况变更）上演示；另对湍流立方体（在智能体循环外运行）作回溯评估。为验证分离设计，将36个记录状态（部分诊断被人为篡改）以两种提示设置回放同一模型。

Result: 无检查结果、仅凭证据判断时，模型在三次重复中均接受了13个可检出故障中的12个；由CFD Forge的检查判定时，无一被接受。两种提示设置下结论不同，但固定规则给出相同判定。湍流立方体案例中求解器收敛且平均阻力稳定，但侧向力振荡仍在增长，回溯评估因此拒绝验收。

Conclusion: 模型的价值在于开放式工作而非最终判定；"模型做事、检查定夺"的分工是迈向可用于高通量设计与主动学习循环的可信自动化CFD的一步。案例文件、模型调用记录与决策均保留以供审计。

Abstract: Setting up, running, and assessing a computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulation requires substantial manual effort, from meshing and solver setup to diagnosis and interpretation, and repeating it across design variants slows engineering iteration. We present CFD Forge, an agentic framework built around OpenFOAM that carries an engineering request through case construction, meshing, solver execution, diagnostics, and reporting. A large language model interprets the request, reads the simulation evidence, identifies likely causes of problems, and proposes actions from a closed set. Automation is only useful if its results can be trusted, so acceptance is not left to the model: deterministic checks, registered for each type of flow, measure explicit criteria and decide. We demonstrate the framework on a converging-diverging nozzle and a two-dimensional forward-facing step, including geometry and operating changes requested in text. A turbulent cube, run outside the agent loop, shows why the checks matter: the solver completed and the mean drag settled, but the lateral-force oscillations were still growing, so a retrospective assessment withheld acceptance. To test this separation, we replayed 36 recorded simulation states, some with deliberately altered diagnostics, through the same model in two setups. Judging from the evidence without the check results, the model accepted 12 of the 13 detectable faults in all three repeats; with CFD Forge's checks deciding, none was accepted. The two setups also differ in prompting, and a fixed rule reached the same verdicts, so the model's value lies in the open-ended work rather than the verdict. Case files, recorded model calls, and decisions are retained for audit. A model that does the work, paired with checks that decide what is accepted, is a step toward automated CFD that can be trusted in high-throughput design and active-learning loops.

</details>


### [91] [Finding a reconnection in a vortex-haystack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14316)
*Abhishek Harikrishnan,Benjamin Musci,Adrien Lopez,Bartosz Protas,Adam Cheminet,Jean Le Bris,Jean-Marc Foucaut,Jean-Philippe Laval,Christophe Cuvier,Pierre Bragança,Bérengère Dubrulle*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出一种结合局部时空正则性指标与涡重联指标的新方法，用于在湍流中检测反向旋转涡重联事件，并在两个分辨率的湍流von Kármán流实验数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向旋转涡重联会导致强涡量放大，常出现在速度场最不规则的区域，但受涡定义模糊和强小尺度涡快速增殖影响，直接在湍流中检测该事件极具挑战。

Method: 方法结合两个工具：一是局部尺度、空间和时间上的正则性指标，用于定位感兴趣时空区域；二是重联指标，利用涡重联中enstrophy从对称面流向碰撞面的性质，并借鉴液晶取向序参量开发新方法以在正确参考系中考察enstrophy流动。

Result: 通过与文献中已有诊断方法比较，给出重联指标的概念验证；在两个分辨率的湍流von Kármán流实验数据上验证，其中一个分辨率可探测Kolmogorov尺度η以下流动。

Conclusion: 结果表明反向旋转涡重联可能是产生η以下尺度运动的潜在机制。

Abstract: Counter-rotating vortex reconnection is a topology-transforming event that leads to strong vorticity amplification and is often associated with the most irregular regions of the velocity field. Detecting such events directly in turbulent flows is a major challenge due to the ambiguity inherent in the definition of a vortex and the rapid proliferation of intense small scale vortices. We develop a novel methodology by combining two tools. The first is a local in scale, space and time regularity indicator that isolates spatio-temporal regions of interest. The second is a reconnection indicator that exploits a well-known property of vortex reconnection namely, that the enstrophy (volume integral of the square of vorticity) flows from the symmetry to the collision plane. To examine the flow of enstrophy in the correct reference frame, a new method inspired by orientational order parameters in liquid crystals is developed. We then show a proof of concept for our reconnection indicator by comparing it with diagnostic methods available in the literature. The method is validated on experimental datasets from a turbulent von Kármán flow at two different resolutions, one of which allows us to probe the flow below the Kolmogorov scale, $η$. Our findings suggest that counter-rotating vortex reconnection could be a potential mechanism for the creation of motions at scales below $η$.

</details>


### [92] [AI Meets Turbulence Modeling: Complex Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14355)
*Sharath S. Girimaji*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以自由度(Re)和非平衡度(SK/ε)为参数的湍流分类图，借助Weaver复杂度框架将湍流划分为不同物理机制区，从而指导AI/ML在湍流建模中的情景化、流场感知式部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI/ML在湍流建模中广受关注，但进展有限，核心原因在于缺乏情景感知的部署策略。数据驱动的ML-RANS闭合模型难以跨流态泛化，而尺度解析模拟(SRS)计算代价高昂，导致ML-RANS常被误用于其有效域之外，ML-SRS又被不必要地用于本可由RANS处理的流动。

Method: 提出一个由自由度(Re)与非平衡度(SK/ε)参数化的湍流分类图(taxonomy map)，并应用Weaver的复杂度框架，将湍流划分为具有不同物理特征的机制区域。

Result: 该分类明确了局部ML-RANS代理模型可泛化的区域，厘清了必须进行显式尺度解析的物理情形，并指出了物理信息神经网络(PINNs)最有效的适用范围。

Conclusion: 该视角建立了一个基于物理的框架，用于在湍流中实现流场感知、恰当的AI/ML部署。

Abstract: Despite widespread enthusiasm, applying artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) to turbulence modeling has yielded only modest progress, primarily due to a lack of situation-aware deployment. Data-driven Reynolds-averaged Navier--Stokes (ML-RANS) closures frequently fail to generalize across diverse flow regimes, while scale-resolving simulations (SRS) remain computationally prohibitive. As a result, ML-RANS is often misapplied outside its domain of validity, and ML-SRS is unnecessarily deployed on flows fully amenable to RANS. To guide optimal AI/ML deployment, we propose a turbulence taxonomy map parameterized by degrees of freedom ($Re$) and the degree of non-equilibrium ($SK/ε$). By applying Weaver's well-recognized complexity framework, we classify turbulence into distinct physical regimes. This categorization identifies where local ML-RANS surrogates can generalize, clarifies where explicit scale resolution is physically mandatory, and highlights where physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are most effective. Ultimately, this perspective establishes a physics-grounded framework for the flow-aware, successful deployment of AI/ML in turbulence.

</details>


### [93] [Surfactant-induced Marangoni stresses and surface viscosity in Faraday instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14983)
*Bochang Wang,C. Ricardo Constante-Amores*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文扩展了两流体Floquet模型，纳入表面活性剂输运、Marangoni应力与表面黏度，研究了不可溶表面活性剂弹性与Boussinesq-Scriven表面黏度对两黏性流体界面参数不稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有参数不稳定性研究多针对洁净界面，缺乏对表面活性剂弹性与表面黏度这两种界面流变机制如何影响失稳起始及模态特性的系统认识。

Method: 将Kumar & Tuckerman (1994)的两流体Floquet公式扩展，加入表面活性剂输运、Marangoni应力与Boussinesq-Scriven表面黏度；分析无驱动重力-毛细模态，并进行波数选择。

Result: 两种机制均提高起始加速度但有本质差异：Marangoni弹性给出非单调阈值，表面黏度则单调上升趋于饱和；二者均改变模态阻尼与固有频率；波数选择使亚谐共振失谐量降低约一个数量级；所选分支上起始加速度变化与模态阻尼变化高度一致，残余失谐次之。

Conclusion: 尽管Marangoni弹性与表面黏度的界面应力机制不同，在强流变极限下二者均抑制界面切向运动，趋于共同的切向不动条件。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of insoluble-surfactant elasticity and Boussinesq-Scriven surface viscosity on the parametric instability of an interface separating two viscous fluids. The two-fluid Floquet formulation of Kumar& Tuckerman (J. Fluid Mech., vol. 279, 1994, pp. 49-68) is extended to include surfactant transport, Marangoni stresses and surface viscosity. For the parameter range considered, both mechanisms increase the onset acceleration relative to the clean interface state, but through distinct responses: Marangoni elasticity produces a non-monotonic threshold, whereas surface viscosity yields a monotonic increase towards saturation. Analysis of the corresponding unforced gravity-capillary modes shows that both mechanisms modify modal damping and natural frequency. Wavenumber selection reduces the detuning from subharmonic resonance by approximately an order of magnitude relative to that evaluated at the clean interface critical wavenumber. Along the selected branches, variations in the onset acceleration closely follow those in modal damping, with residual detuning playing a secondary role. Despite their distinct interfacial stress mechanisms, both Marangoni elasticity and surface viscosity suppress tangential interfacial motion in the strong-rheology limit, approaching a common tangentially immobilized condition.

</details>


### [94] [Nonlinear waves and conservative transport in soluble-surfactant falling films on compliant substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15048)
*Sanghasri Mukhopadhyay,Asim Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究含可溶性表面活性剂的液膜在倾斜柔性基底上流动的有限振幅波，采用守恒加权残差模型耦合液-板运动与表面活性剂输运，并用非线性计算考察行进态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液膜在可变形（柔性）基底上的流动涉及液-固耦合与表面活性剂输运，其有限振幅波行为及基底性质、Marangoni耦合的影响尚不清晰，需要建立能兼顾黏性与附加质量的低维模型并量化其可靠性。

Method: 采用守恒加权残差模型描述液体与弹簧支撑板的耦合运动，纳入体相与自由表面的表面活性剂输运，保留壁面运动引起的黏性项，压力闭合在均匀平移下恢复流体附加质量；与耦合Orr-Sommerfeld问题对比验证；随后进行非线性计算考察行进态、移动参考系重构、载荷-功预算及控制计算。

Result: Re=25时膜模态峰值增长率与耦合Orr-Sommerfeld结果相差约1%；沿给定刚度-阻尼路径，基底位移下降超过四个数量级而波速变化小于1%；自由表面高程与液膜厚度极值变化趋势不同；Marangoni耦合降低表面活性剂局域化并削弱波与基底响应；移动参考系显示波峰附近存在回流；毛细压力是两种柔性支撑下基底功的主要净来源；固定阻尼改变刚度时阻尼损失变化显著而变形变化较小。

Conclusion: 该守恒加权残差模型能以约1%精度捕捉线性稳定性，并适用于非线性行进态分析；表面高度不能可靠指示柔性支撑上的液膜减薄，Marangoni耦合整体抑制响应，基底功主要由毛细压力驱动，刚度对阻尼损失的影响远大于对变形的影响。

Abstract: We investigate finite-amplitude waves in a liquid film containing a soluble surfactant as it flows down an inclined compliant substrate. The coupled motion of the liquid and a spring-backed plate is described by a conservative weighted-residual model, which also accounts for surfactant transport in the bulk and at the free surface. The reduction retains viscous terms arising from wall motion, and its pressure closure recovers fluid added mass under uniform translation. Comparison with the coupled Orr-Sommerfeld problem gives a difference of approximately $1\%$ in the peak growth rate of the film mode at $Re=25$. Nonlinear computations are then used to examine travelling states and their dependence on substrate properties and surfactant coupling. Along the prescribed stiffness-damping path, substrate displacement decreases by more than four orders of magnitude, while wave speed changes by less than $1\%$. The extrema of free-surface elevation and liquid thickness vary differently, so surface height alone does not reliably indicate film thinning over a deformable support. In the cases studied, Marangoni coupling reduces surfactant localisation and weakens the wave and substrate response. Moving-frame reconstruction reveals recirculation near the wave crest and distinguishes propagation of the deformation pattern from transverse substrate motion. The load-work budget identifies capillary pressure as the principal net source of substrate work for the two compliant supports considered. A control calculation in which stiffness is varied at fixed damping shows a substantial change in damping loss but a comparatively small change in deformation.

</details>


### [95] [Physics Foundation Models for Efficient Control of Fluid Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15173)
*Mahindra Rautela,Shaifalee Saxena,Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文探究将预训练于PDE预测的物理基础模型（PFM）迁移到闭环流体流动控制的可行性，通过在冻结的PFM表征上附加轻量Gaussian actor-critic头并用PPO训练策略，在二维Kolmogorov流环境中验证其控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基础模型已在PDE预测等任务中展现出强大的表征学习能力，但其学习到的表征能否直接迁移到闭环流体控制任务尚不明确。本文旨在探索自监督下一帧预测预训练所获得的表征对下游控制任务的可迁移性。

Method: 构建基于PFM的控制器：将轻量的Gaussian actor头和critic头附加到冻结的预训练表征上，使用PPO算法训练策略。在HydroGym的二维Kolmogorov流环境中评估，观测为64×64与128×128分辨率的涡量场，雷诺数设为Re=100与200，并与使用相同PPO框架训练的MLP基线策略进行对比。

Result: 在各设置下，PFM控制器相比MLP基线策略持续获得更高奖励，同时所需执行的动作幅度更小，表明其在提升控制性能的同时降低了控制代价。

Conclusion: 结果初步表明，通过自监督下一帧预测学习到的表征可直接迁移到闭环流体流动控制任务，为物理基础模型在控制领域的复用提供了证据。

Abstract: We investigate whether a physics foundation model (PFM) pretrained for PDE forecasting can be repurposed for closed-loop fluid-flow control. We construct a PFM-based controller by attaching lightweight Gaussian actor and critic heads to frozen pretrained representations and train the policy using proximal policy optimization (PPO). We evaluate the approach on HydroGym's two-dimensional Kolmogorov-flow environment using vorticity observations at \(64\times64\) and \(128\times128\) resolutions and Reynolds numbers \(\mathrm{Re}=100\) and \(200\). Across these settings, we compare the frozen PFM controller against a baseline MLP policy trained with the same PPO framework. The PFM controller achieves consistently higher reward while requiring smaller executed action magnitudes over training, indicating improved control performance with reduced control effort. These results provide initial evidence that representations learned through self-supervised next-frame prediction can be transferred directly to closed-loop fluid flow control. Project page: https://mahindrautela.github.io/morph.

</details>


### [96] [Numerical Modeling of a Metallic Alloy Droplet Impact, Solidification, and Macrosegregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15381)
*Ehsan Amani,Ali Moinirad*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 首次研究传质对合金液滴撞击、凝固及宏观偏析(MS)的影响，建立并验证多物理场耦合模型，提出浓度通量修正算法守恒溶质质量，揭示传质与溶质输运对凝固薄片动力学预测的改进作用及关键偏析区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往所有研究均忽略传质作用，导致对凝固薄片动力学的预测与实验观测不一致；本文旨在首次系统研究传质对合金液滴撞击、凝固和溶质非均匀性(宏观偏析)的影响。

Method: 建立包含多相流流体动力学、润湿、共轭传热、相变和传质全耦合的多物理场模型，并在多个基准案例上仔细验证；提出浓度通量修正算法，在数值离散层面守恒液滴内溶质质量；用验证后的模型探究合金液滴的撞击与凝固过程。

Result: 考虑传质与溶质输运后，凝固薄片动力学预测得到改善且与实验观测一致；该改进源于流动对糊状区厚度的敏感性，而糊状区厚度通过固相线和液相线温度受局部溶质浓度调控。MS图谱分析识别出若干易引发裂纹等缺陷的关键偏析区：薄片内由撞击中期穿过糊状区的强烈平流形成的长负偏析脉、由钩状形成导致的薄片边缘正偏析区，以及由薄片冲刷维持的富溶质熔体积累而形成的边缘顶部主要正偏析区。

Conclusion: 传质与溶质输运对合金液滴撞击凝固行为的预测不可或缺，所建模型与浓度通量修正算法能有效再现实验观测并识别关键宏观偏析区，为理解裂纹等缺陷萌生提供依据。

Abstract: For the first time, we study the effects of mass transfer on alloy droplet impact, solidification, and solute non-homogeneity or MacroSegregation (MS). For this purpose, a multi-physics model, including fully-coupled hydrodynamics of multiphase flow, wetting, conjugate heat transfer, phase change, and mass transfer, is established and validated carefully across several benchmarks. In addition, a concentration-flux-correction algorithm is proposed to conserve the solute mass within the droplet at the numerical discretization level. The validated model is subsequently used to explore the impact and solidification of an alloy droplet. The results show that accounting for mass transfer and solute transport improves the prediction of the dynamics of a solidifying splat, in a consistent manner with experimental observations, while this effect has been neglected in all previous studies. The analysis demonstrates that this improvement results from the flow sensitivity to the mushy region thickness, which is regulated by local solute concentration through the solidus and liquidus temperatures. Furthermore, analyses of the MS map identify several critical segregation regions most susceptible to defect initiation, such as cracking, including a long negatively segregated vein within the lamella, formed by the severe advective flow through the mush in the middle stages of the impact, a positively segregated region near the rim of the splat, appearing as a result of hook formation, and a major positively segregated zone at the top of the rim, which develops following the accumulation of the solute-rich melt sustained by washout from the lamella.

</details>


### [97] [Local sensing of gas microflows using a levitated nanoparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15462)
*Maxime Debiossac,David Grass,Daniel R. Ladiges,John E. Sader,Markus Aspelmeyer,Nikolai Kiesel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作利用悬浮纳米颗粒作为微创探针，高空间分辨地测量微管道内气压梯度下的局部压力与流速，覆盖滑移流至自由分子流多个流区，并观测到高压比下的非线性压力分布，与动理学理论及DSMC计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微尺度几何中气体流动的局部测量长期受限于微机械传感器难以植入，缺乏高空间分辨的原位探测手段。

Method: 以光悬浮（levitated）纳米颗粒作为最小侵入探针，在承受压力梯度的典型微管道中成像式测量局部气压与气流速度，并与动理学理论预测及直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）计算对比。

Result: 实现了跨越滑移流到自由分子流多个流区的非平衡气体输运表征；直接观测到高压力比下非线性压力分布的出现，结果与动理学理论及DSMC计算吻合。

Conclusion: 悬浮纳米颗粒可作为强大且可重构的微通道气体传感探针，在微技术、微化学与生物工程中具有应用前景。

Abstract: Local measurements of gas flow in microscale geometries remain challenging due to the difficulty of micromechanical sensors implantation. Here, we use a levitated nanoparticle as a minimally invasive probe to map, with high spatial resolution, both the local pressure and velocity gas flow for a canonical example - a micropipe subjected to a pressure gradient. This technique enables characterization of nonequilibrium gas transport across several flow regimes, from slip flow to free-molecular flow. We directly observe the emergence of a nonlinear pressure distribution at large pressure ratios and obtain agreement with kinetic-theory predictions and Direct Simulation Monte Carlo calculations. These results introduce levitated nanoparticles as powerful and reconfigurable probes for microchannel gas sensing, with applications in microtechnology, micro-chemistry, and bioengineering.

</details>


### [98] [Identification of effective transport physics and optimal feedback control: Experimental demonstration on a 1600-fan wind generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15547)
*Tamir Shaqarin,Izz Al-Manasra,TianXiang Zhang,Andrea Ianiro,Stefano Discetti,Bernd R. Noack*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出基于物理的DIDO系统建模与闭环最优控制框架，并在1600风扇FAWG上验证，开环预测误差11-20%，闭环控制误差低于2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式输入分布式输出（DIDO）系统广泛存在于执行器产生空间分布物理场、分布式传感器监测该场的工程场景中。由于系统维度高、输入与输出间存在强空间耦合，建立可物理解释且支持实时最优控制的模型十分困难。

Method: 1）从候选输运模型中选取有效偏微分方程（PDE），在执行器网格上离散化，得到最小网格表示；2）对PDE求闭式解，得到仅含四个可物理解释参数的输入-输出算子；3）将该算子嵌入考虑执行器限幅的迭代约束二次规划中，实现闭环最优控制。

Result: 在全球最高分辨率风扇阵列风发生器（FAWG）的40×40内部区域（1600个独立控制风扇、36个分布式速度测点）上验证：模型以11-20%的归一化平均绝对预测误差描述1600个执行器与速度场间的空间交互；闭环控制器在少于10次迭代内复现全部三种二维设定速度场，归一化平均绝对误差低于2.3%。

Conclusion: 该物理建模与控制框架能有效处理高维强耦合DIDO系统，兼具可解释性、低参数化与快速收敛能力，适用于需要在实时闭环中调控空间分布场的工程应用。

Abstract: Distributed-input distributed-output (DIDO) systems arise in numerous engineering applications where large numbers of actuators generate spatially distributed physical fields that are monitored by distributed sensors. Developing physically interpretable models that enable real-time optimal control of these systems remains challenging because of the high dimensionality and strong spatial coupling between inputs and outputs. In this work, we propose a physics-based framework for modeling and closed-loop optimal control of DIDO systems from experimental input-output data. First, an effective partial differential equation (PDE) is selected among candidate transport models and discretized on the actuator grid to obtain a minimum-grid representation. Second, the PDE is solved in closed form, which yields an input-output operator with only four physically interpretable parameters. Third, the operator is incorporated into an iterative, constrained quadratic program that accounts for the actuator limits. The framework is demonstrated on the inner 40 x 40 section of the world's highest-resolution Fan Array Wind Generator (FAWG), comprising 1600 independently controlled fans with 36 distributed velocity measurements. The model describes the spatial interaction between the 1600 actuators and the measured velocity field with normalized mean absolute prediction errors of 11-20% for representative actuation patterns. The closed-loop controller reproduces all three prescribed two-dimensional velocity fields with a normalized mean absolute error below 2.3% in fewer than 10 iterations.

</details>


### [99] [Nanofluid transport in Taylor bubble flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15812)
*Fahad Al-Gburi,Faysal Khaleel,Yulong Ding,Jason Stafford*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文建立了欧拉-拉格朗日框架，研究非等温Taylor气泡流中纳米颗粒悬浮液的传热与流动耦合机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Taylor气泡流与纳米颗粒悬浮液在非等温化学合成和微流控中常见，但其热-水动力耦合相互作用缺乏完整机理认识。

Method: 开发欧拉-拉格朗日数值框架，捕捉双向动量与能量交换及非等温下的颗粒相互作用，考察Poiseuille流与Taylor流两类问题。

Result: 布朗运动具有双重作用：增强局部颗粒-流体能量交换，同时驱动热泳漂移远离热壁，粒径低于100 nm时加剧；φ~10^-2时Taylor流偏离无颗粒时的Nu与无量纲段塞长度标度关系，伴随气泡拓扑改变；visco-inertial区(Re>500)由水动力、布朗和界面力与气泡/段塞拓扑共同决定Nu正负；界面短程力与段塞对流协同改变局部颗粒浓度与能量交换；当L̃_s≤1.6且Re增大时对流强度改善颗粒再分布。

Conclusion: 传统混合介质方法可能不适用，需考虑布朗、界面力与拓扑耦合的多尺度机理。

Abstract: Taylor bubble flows with nanoparticle suspensions are regularly encountered in non-isothermal chemical synthesis and microfluidics. Despite this, a complete mechanistic insight into the coupled thermal and hydrodynamic interactions remains unresolved. Here, a Eulerian-Lagrangian framework has been developed to address this, capturing two-way momentum, energy exchange, and nanoparticle interactions under non-isothermal conditions. We find that Brownian motion acts in two opposing ways, enhancing local particle-fluid energy exchange yet driving a thermophoretic drift away from the heated wall that strengthens as particle diameter is reduced below 100 nm. At particle volume fractions of $φ\sim 10^{-2}$, Taylor flow departs from the Nusselt scaling with dimensionless slug length, $\tilde{L}_s$, observed for particle-free flows. This coincides with alterations to bubble topology that co-benefit nanoparticle momentum and energy exchange with the carrier fluid. Examining both Poiseuille and Taylor flow problems, we observe contrasting, often opposing dimensionless heat transport behaviours, questioning the suitability of traditionally accepted mixture approaches. This work reveals that, in the visco-inertial regime ($Re > 500$), it is the interplay of hydrodynamic, Brownian, and interfacial forces, combined with the Taylor bubble and slug flow topology, which determine positive or negative $Nu$ outcomes. Interestingly, short-range particle forces at the liquid-gas interface work synergistically with the convective motion in the slug to alter local particle concentration and energy exchanges within downstream thin film and near-wall regions. The intensity of this convective flow was found to impact both slug and film transport phenomena, improving the redistribution of nanoparticles when $\tilde{L}_s \leq 1.6$ and with increasing $Re$.

</details>


### [100] [Capillary-gravity wave resistance of a translating, pulsating source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15859)
*Haim Diamant,Elie Raphael*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究沿毛细-重力界面作匀速直线运动的扰动源，推导出线源的平均波阻闭式解。结果表明，只要存在频率调制Ω≠0，平均波阻在任何非零速度下都严格为正，从而消除了稳态下的无阻（亚阈值）区间；跨辐射转变处出现可积共振峰，并给出其指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态匀速直线运动的源在毛细-重力界面上仅当速度超过阈值c_min时才辐射波并感受到波阻，低于阈值则无波、无阻。对源振幅施加频率调制可将激励移至边带对kV±Ω，从而引入稳态问题中不存在的共振条件，使辐射在低于稳态阈值时即可发生。本文旨在定量刻画这种调制所导致的平均波阻行为，特别是无阻区间是否消失。

Method: 对毛细-重力界面上的线源施加频率为Ω的振幅调制，将激励分析在边带对kV±Ω上；据此推导平均波阻⟨R⟩的闭式表达式，并分析其在辐射转变处（群速度等于源速度）的共振峰结构及指数，包括两支转变支合并的尖点处；同时考察亚阈值下⟨R⟩随Ω的标度律及表面张力的作用。

Result: 平均波阻⟨R⟩具有闭式解，且在任何非零速度V下都严格为正；只要Ω≠0，无阻的亚阈值区间便消失。在辐射转变处（群速度与源速度匹配）⟨R⟩出现可积共振峰，本文给出其指数，并给出两支转变支合并的尖点处的相应指数。在亚阈值下，固定V时⟨R⟩以Ω^5从零上升。表面张力对⟨R⟩至关重要，因为它主导短波、占优的毛细尺度波，若忽略表面张力，⟨R⟩在几乎整个速度-频率平面上都会被错误描述。

Conclusion: 频率调制破坏了稳态亚阈值无阻区间：只要Ω≠0，平均波阻对任意非零V都为正，无阻状态不再存在。辐射转变处的共振峰为可积型，其指数（含尖点）已被解析给出；亚阈值下⟨R⟩∝Ω^5。表面张力主导毛细尺度波，是准确刻画⟨R⟩的必要因素，忽略它会在几乎整个速度-频率平面上产生错误结果。

Abstract: A disturbance in steady rectilinear translation along a capillary-gravity interface radiates waves only above a minimum speed $c_{min}$, where it experiences a wave resistance; below this threshold it generates no waves, and the response is merely a localised deformation of the interface around the source, free of wave resistance. Modulating the source amplitude at a frequency $Ω$ shifts the forcing to the sideband pair $kV\pmΩ$, where $k$ is the wavenumber and $V$ is the translation speed, opening resonance conditions absent from the steady problem; radiation then sets in below the steady threshold $c_{min}$. Here we derive the mean wave resistance $\langle R\rangle$ of a line source in closed form, and show it to be strictly positive at every nonzero speed $V$: the drag-free below-threshold regime disappears for any $Ω\neq 0$. Crossing the radiation transitions---where the group velocity matches the source speed---produces integrable resonant peaks in $\langle R\rangle$, whose exponents we derive, including at the cusp where two transition branches merge. Below threshold, where a steadily moving source feels no drag, $\langle R\rangle$ rises from zero as $Ω^5$ (at fixed $V$). Throughout, surface tension is essential to $\langle R\rangle$: because it controls the short, dominant capillary-scale waves, discarding it misrepresents $\langle R\rangle$ over almost the entire speed-frequency plane.

</details>


### [101] [Nonlocal generalization of the Chapman-Enskog method and hydrodynamic equations. Nonlocal vorticity transport in a turbulent medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16014)
*A. B. Kukushkin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将广义Chapman-Enskog方法与非局域黏性应力张量推广到任意速度与温度梯度情形，导出非局域涡量输运方程，并得到符合Richardson t³律的长自由程涡量载流子速度分布幂律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Fluid Dyn 61, 20 (2026)）仅在任意速度梯度下建立了广义Chapman-Enskog方法及Navier-Stokes方程中的非局域（非扩散、空间积分型）黏性应力张量，尚未涵盖温度梯度；而切向间断与分离流等流动的刻画需要同时考虑速度与温度的任意梯度。

Method: 把广义Chapman-Enskog方法推广到速度和温度梯度均任意的情况，构造相应的非局域黏性应力张量；对不可压缩介质进一步推导非局域涡量输运方程，并与等离子体和气体中共振辐射输运的Biberman-Holstein方程作结构类比；通过求解长自由程涡量载流子的速度分布函数，确定其幂律形式。

Result: 所得方程简化了切向间断和分离流的描述；对不可压缩介质导出的非局域涡量输运方程在结构上与等离子体和气体中共振辐射输运的Biberman-Holstein方程完全相同；所确定的长自由程涡量载流子速度分布函数呈幂律形式，并满足湍流液气介质中粒子对相对分离均方值的经验Richardson t³律。

Conclusion: 该工作将非局域黏性输运理论从速度梯度推广到速度与温度梯度并存的一般情形，揭示了涡量输运与共振辐射输运的数学同构性，并给出与Richardson t³律一致的长自由程涡量载流子分布，为切向间断和分离流提供了更简洁的描述框架。

Abstract: The results from [Fluid Dyn 61, 20 (2026)], concerning the generalized Chapman-Enskog method and a nonlocal (i.e., non-diffusive, spatially integral) viscous stress tensor in the Navier-Stokes equation, are extended to the case of arbitrary gradients of not only hydrodynamic velocity but also temperature. The resulting equations simplify the description of tangential discontinuities and separated flows. For the specific case of an incompressible medium, a nonlocal vorticity transport equation is derived that is structurally identical to the Biberman-Holstein equation for resonant radiation transport in plasmas and gases. A power-law form has been determined for the velocity distribution function of long-free-path vorticity carriers that satisfies empirical Richardson t^3 law for the mean square of relative separation of a pair of particles in a turbulent liquid or gaseous medium.

</details>


### [102] [Wave-driven geostrophic vortices in rotating turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15863)
*Vladimir Parfenyev,Sergey Vergeles*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过直接数值模拟研究旋转湍流中惯性波与地转涡旋的相互作用，发现波-涡角动量交换机制控制大尺度地转流的维持，且旋转速率过快反而抑制湍流的二维化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明大尺度地转涡旋在旋转湍流中得以维持的物理机制，特别是惯性波在其中扮演的角色，以及旋转速率对湍流二维化的实际影响。

Method: 对旋转湍流进行直接数值模拟（DNS），将惯性波产生的雷诺应力与 RANS 大尺度流平衡进行对比，并开展波场分析以考察地转涡旋对惯性波的选择性反射与吸收。

Result: ①惯性波产生的雷诺应力与 RANS 大尺度流平衡一致，并再现了气旋-反气旋不对称性（气旋方位速度更强、核心更紧凑）；②地转涡旋依据角动量符号选择性反射和吸收惯性波，吸收可通过反射点前的临界层形成或局部波数增大带来的黏性耗散增强实现；③旋转增强时参与共振吸收的惯性波减少，向涡旋的角动量输运减弱，地转流被削弱，地转模态能量占比随旋转速率呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 地转涡旋与惯性波的角动量交换是维持大尺度地转流的关键机制；足够快的旋转会抑制而非增强湍流二维化，地转模态能量占比与旋转速率呈非单调关系。

Abstract: We perform direct numerical simulations of rotating turbulence to investigate the mechanisms responsible for the maintenance of large-scale geostrophic vortices. We confirm that the Reynolds stresses generated by inertial waves are consistent with the Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes balance of the large-scale flow and demonstrate the predicted cyclone-anticyclone asymmetry, with cyclones exhibiting stronger azimuthal velocities and more compact cores. Wave-field analysis provides evidence that geostrophic vortices selectively reflect and absorb inertial waves according to the sign of their angular momentum. Wave absorption occurs either through the formation of a critical layer ahead of the reflection point or through enhanced viscous dissipation associated with increasing local wavenumber. As rotation increases, fewer inertial waves undergo resonant absorption, reducing angular-momentum transfer to the vortices and weakening the geostrophic flow. Consequently, the fraction of energy contained in geostrophic modes depends non-monotonically on the rotation rate, demonstrating that sufficiently rapid rotation can ultimately suppress, rather than enhance, turbulence two-dimensionalization.

</details>


### [103] [A ghost-cell immersed boundary method for the shallow water equations on Cartesian grids: mass conservation and wetting and drying over variable topography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16148)
*Yuta Yaegashi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种用于变底二维浅水方程的幽灵单元浸入边界法，通过将连续方程改为切割单元形式实现质量守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幽灵单元法在壁面处存在质量泄漏问题，需要找到一种鲁棒且守恒的处理方式。

Method: 在笛卡尔网格上采用显式Godunov型有限体积格式（非分裂二阶MUSCL、HLLC通量、静水重构），测试了八种幽灵值构造方式及切割单元形式的连续方程，最终方案在镜像点处双线性插值幽灵值，排除固体和干节点并重新归一化权重，同时以切割单元形式求解连续方程而保持动量方程不变。

Result: 该方案精度与未修改的幽灵单元法相当，且能保持岸线穿过浸入壁面的静水状态，通过七个测试问题（含斜坡床面干湿变化和实验室溃坝）得到验证。

Conclusion: 仅修改连续方程即可实现质量守恒，避免了完整切割单元法所需的动量方程切割单元处理（壁面压力、单元合并）。

Abstract: A ghost-cell immersed boundary method is proposed for the two-dimensional shallow water equations with a variable bed (topography), solved with an explicit Godunov-type finite volume scheme (unsplit second-order MUSCL, HLLC flux, hydrostatic reconstruction) on Cartesian grids. Ghost-cell methods leak mass through the wall. To find a robust and conservative treatment, eight constructions of the ghost values and a cut-cell form of the continuity equation were applied to seven test problems, which include wetting and drying on sloping beds and a laboratory dam break. In the resulting scheme, the ghost values are interpolated bilinearly at the image point; solid and dry nodes are excluded, and the weights of the remaining nodes are renormalized. Mass is conserved by solving the continuity equation in cut-cell form, while the momentum equations are left unchanged. The proposed scheme is about as accurate as the ghost-cell method without this modification and preserves a lake at rest whose shoreline crosses an immersed wall. Because only the continuity equation is modified, it avoids the treatment of the momentum equations on cut cells (wall pressure, cell merging) that a full cut-cell method requires.

</details>


### [104] [Planar intrusions from a narrow, jet-like source in a linearly stratified ambient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16011)
*Hai Duc Vu,Murray Rudman,Anja Slim*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 对线性分层环境中的平面侵入流进行二维与三维直接数值模拟，揭示其经历前导涡偶、射流正弦不稳定性及浮力控制楔形三个演化阶段；三维效应在近源区增强混合并使下游侵入接近宽源相似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究从狭窄连续源注入、在浮力中性层进入线性分层环境的平面侵入流的演化机制，尤其是二维不稳定性在三维流场中的作用，以及三维性对源区混合与下游侵入行为的影响。

Method: 采用二维和三维直接数值模拟，对连续供给的狭窄源在线性分层、中性浮力层高度注入的平面侵入流进行模拟，并将射流不稳定性增长率与波长同无粘无分层平面射流理论对比。

Result: 演化经历三阶段：层流前导涡偶以恒定速度推进并伴随静脉曲张波列；尾随射流发生正弦不稳定性，其初始增长率和波长与基于局部射流的无粘无分层平面射流理论吻合；流场最终松弛为面积随时间线性增长的浮力控制楔形。三维模拟中，平面不稳定性仍主导转捩，但三维性将近源区重塑为缓慢演化的强烈混合区，根据源宽不同额外卷吸源通量的20%至100%。以该有效通量重新标度后，下游侵入较好地坍缩到宽源侵入的相似解上。

Conclusion: 平面不稳定性组织着二维与三维侵入的转捩过程，而三维性主要通过增强近源混合、提高有效源通量来改变下游行为，修正通量后下游侵入可归入宽源相似解框架。

Abstract: We present two and three dimensional direct numerical simulations of planar intrusions injected from a continuously supplied, narrow source into a linearly stratified ambient at the level of neutral buoyancy. The evolution passes through three phases. A laminar leading vortex dipole first advances at constant speed with a varicose wave train behind it; the trailing jet then undergoes a sinuous instability whose initial growth rate and wavelength agree with inviscid unstratified planar-jet theory evaluated on the local jet; the flow finally relaxes into a buoyancy-controlled wedge whose area grows linearly in time. Three-dimensional simulations show that the transition remains organized by the planar instability, while three-dimensionality reshapes the near-source region into a slowly evolving, actively mixing zone that entrains an additional twenty to one hundred percent of the source flux depending on source width. Rescaled with this measured effective flux, the downstream intrusion collapses reasonably well onto the similarity solution for a wide-source intrusion.

</details>


### [105] [Fingering instability of thin films with an inclined contact line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16380)
*Ze-Hui Gao,Er-Qiang Li,Peng Gao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 在润滑理论框架下，采用数值与渐近方法研究倾斜接触线重力驱动薄膜的线性稳定性，揭示接触线倾斜对指进不稳定性的抑制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜流动的指进不稳定性是工业过程中获得均匀稳定薄膜的重大障碍。已有研究多针对水平接触线，而实际工艺中接触线常呈倾斜状态，其对稳定性的影响尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究接触线倾斜的作用。

Method: 在润滑理论框架下，对含倾斜接触线的重力驱动薄膜进行线性稳定性分析；采用Greenspan滑移模型正则化接触线奇异性；结合数值求解与长波极限下的渐近分析，考察倾角对增长率、最不稳定波数及模态性质的影响。

Result: 接触线倾斜略微增强长波不稳定性，但显著抑制展向有限波长扰动；随倾角增大，最不稳定波数向长波区移动。倾斜接触线引入复特征值，产生行波模态，而水平接触线对应驻波模态。渐近分析证实长波不稳定性的增强。

Conclusion: 接触线倾斜的稳定性效应源于特征模态畸变与附加流向通量两个物理机制，结果为控制薄膜指进不稳定性、优化涂布等工业工艺提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The fingering instability of thin-film flow is a significant obstacle to producing uniform and stable films in industrial processes. In the framework of lubrication theory, we investigate the linear stability of a gravity-driven thin film with an inclined contact line using numerical and asymptotic methods. The contact-line singularity is regularized by imposing the Greenspan slip model. As demonstrated by the results, although the contact-line inclination slightly enhances long-wavelength instability, it significantly suppresses the growth of spanwise finite-wavelength perturbations. Moreover, the most unstable wavenumber shifts toward the long-wave region as the inclination angle increases. The inclination of the contact line introduces complex eigenvalues, leading to traveling-wave modes, in contrast to the standing-wave modes observed for horizontal contact lines. We also performed asymptotic analysis in the limit of small wavenumbers, confirming the enhanced long-wave instability. The physical mechanisms underlying these stability characteristics are elucidated, revealing that the effects of contact-line inclination come from the eigenmode distortion and additional streamwise flux.

</details>


### [106] [Onset condition of the ramified-jet regime: an experimental and theoretical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16585)
*Bor Zupan,Jernej Kušar,Božidar Šarler*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与理论，揭示电流体动力学中气体聚焦液射流向边缘发射液片转变的机理，提出以电毛细对称破缺为核心的临界判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气体聚焦液射流在电场作用下向边缘发射液片（rim-emitting sheet）的转变机制尚不明确，缺乏可预测起始条件的物理判据，因此需要实验与理论结合来阐明转变机理。

Method: 采用超高速成像实验，考察施加电压、气/液流量及液体物性对液片形成与破碎的影响；并建立一阶理论模型，假设界面等电位、忽略有限电荷弛豫、流体动力学扰动与非线性演化，在导电圆柱射流上平衡去稳定的麦克斯韦压力扰动与恢复性毛细压力扰动。

Result: 乙醇可形成充分发展的准稳态液片，异丙醇仅出现初始液片；升高电压促进液片拉伸、产生更细更多边缘射流并收窄喷雾；提高气流量使转变位置上移至喷嘴并产生更细结构，提高液流量则生成更大液片与更厚边缘。模型识别出m=2椭圆方位角模式为首个不稳定模式，预测临界电邦德数为3，预测起始电压与实测值偏差在20%以内，且阈值与已发表的枝化电喷雾观察一致。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持电毛细对称破缺是转变起始的机制，所提模型为预测电射流向边缘发射液片转变提供了紧凑且有物理依据的判据。

Abstract: This study experimentally and theoretically investigates the electrically induced transition of a gas-focused liquid jet into a rim-emitting sheet. Ultra-high-speed imaging was used to examine the effects of applied voltage, gas and liquid flow rates, and liquid properties on sheet formation and breakup. Fully developed, quasi-stable sheets formed with ethanol, whereas isopropanol exhibited only initial sheet formation. Increasing voltage promoted sheet stretching, finer and more numerous rim jets, and a narrower spray. Higher gas flow rates shifted the transition towards the nozzle and produced finer structures, while higher liquid flow rates generated larger sheets with thicker rims. A first-order theoretical model assumes an equipotential interface and neglects finite charge relaxation, hydrodynamic perturbations, and nonlinear evolution. It balances destabilizing Maxwell-pressure perturbations against restoring capillary-pressure perturbations on a conducting cylindrical jet. The model identifies the elliptic azimuthal mode, m = 2, as the first unstable mode leading to sheet formation and predicts a critical electric Bond number of 3. Predicted onset voltages agree with measurements within 20%, and the threshold was consistent with published ramified-electrospray observations. The agreement with experiments supports electrocapillary symmetry breaking as the mechanism of onset and provides a compact, physically grounded criterion for predicting the transition from electrified jets to rim-emitting sheets.

</details>


### [107] [Versatile two-layer model for thermohydraulic prediction of microchannel heat sinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16716)
*Chih-Hsiang Chen,Kentaro Yaji*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种通用两层模型（VTLM），将微通道热沉的三维共轭传热问题降维为两个耦合二维场，通过水力和热学封闭关系计入通道高度方向效应，精度优于现有两层模型，并给出筛选准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维共轭传热仿真虽能提供微通道热沉的详细预测，但在评估大量几何结构与运行工况时计算成本过高，需要一种兼具精度与效率的降维建模方法。

Method: VTLM将三维问题简化为两个耦合的二维场，同时保留流道与翅片的平面内几何；对未解析的通道高度方向，通过水力与热学封闭关系（壁面摩擦、流体侧与翅片侧的层间传热贡献）加以表征，封闭项由代表性通道几何、流动发展状态与翅片构型确定。模型在较宽通道高宽比与工况范围（含均匀与非均匀翅片布置）下与三维参考模型对比评估；并进一步测试带翅片的渐扩—渐缩通道。

Result: 相比现有两层模型，VTLM与三维参考结果的整体一致性更好；预测精度主要受流动发展状态与通道高宽比控制，据此提出面向所考察直通道构型的保守筛选准则；带翅片渐扩—渐缩通道的评估进一步支持VTLM对存在突变宽度变化通道的适用性。

Conclusion: VTLM为所考察的微通道热沉构型范围提供了一种无需显式解析通道高度方向的降维热—水力分析框架。

Abstract: Three-dimensional conjugate heat-transfer simulations provide detailed predictions for microchannel heat sinks but can be computationally demanding when many geometries and operating conditions are evaluated. This study develops a versatile two-layer model (VTLM) that reduces the three-dimensional problem to two coupled two-dimensional fields while retaining the in-plane geometry of the flow passages and fins. Effects associated with the unresolved channel-height direction are incorporated through hydraulic and thermal closures that account for wall friction and the fluid- and fin-side contributions to interlayer heat transfer. The closures are evaluated from the representative channel geometry, flow-development condition, and fin configuration. The VTLM was assessed against three-dimensional reference models across broad ranges of channel aspect ratios and operating conditions, including uniform and nonuniform fin arrangements. Compared with the existing two-layer models, the VTLM showed improved overall agreement with the three-dimensional reference results. The results showed that prediction accuracy was governed mainly by the flow-development condition and channel aspect ratio, from which a conservative screening guideline was proposed for the examined straight-channel configurations. The assessment of finned expansion--contraction channels further supported the applicability of the VTLM to the investigated passages with abrupt width changes. Overall, the VTLM provides a reduced-dimensional framework for thermohydraulic analysis across the range of microchannel heat-sink configurations examined in this study without explicitly resolving the channel-height direction.

</details>


### [108] [Second-order fluids and the choice of objective derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16917)
*Maxwell C. Marsh,Randy H. Ewoldt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将二阶流体本构模型推广到包含多种客观时间导数的形式，利用Gordon–Schowalter导数研究导数选择对预测的影响，并识别出不依赖于该选择的普适特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶流体模型中时间导数的选择往往带有主观性，而用通用系数表述模型可能导致对流动预测的误读，因此需要厘清哪些预测依赖于导数选择、哪些具有普适性。

Method: 采用Gordon–Schowalter导数（其子集包括上对流、下对流和共旋/Jaumann–Zaremba导数）对二阶流体本构模型进行推广，并分别用通用系数以及零剪切材料函数或末端松弛时间两种方式表述模型。

Result: 主观选择不同的时间导数会改变模型预测；用通用系数表述时模型结果非普适，易导致对流动预测的误解；而改用零剪切材料函数或末端松弛时间表述时，可获得普适且稳健的表示，并能识别出不依赖于导数选择的普适特征。

Conclusion: 二阶流体模型的普适性取决于其表述方式：应避免使用通用系数，而采用零剪切材料函数或末端松弛时间进行表述，以获得不受时间导数选择影响的稳健预测。

Abstract: We generalize the second-order fluid constitutive model to accommodate a range of possible frame indifferent time derivatives. Using the Gordon--Schowalter derivative, which contains the upper-convected, lower-convected, and corotational (Jaumann-Zaremba) derivatives as subsets, we show how the subjective choice of derivative may change model predictions and we identify features that remain universal independent of this choice. Writing these models in terms of generic coefficients is non-universal and may lead to misinterpretations of flow predictions. However, writing the models in terms of zero-shear material functions or terminal relaxation time reveals universal and robust representations.

</details>


### [109] [DeboRheo v2.0: Improved Robustness for Geometrical VOF Simulations of Viscoelastic Two-Phase Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17082)
*Daulet Izbassarov,Matthias Niethammer,Rahul Subburaj,Dieter Bothe,Francesco Romanò,Renjie Hao,Metin Muradoglu,Tomislav Maric*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: DeboRheo v2.0：基于OpenFOAM的非结构、几何非分裂VOF粘弹性两相流框架，改进界面粘度处理，提升大粘度对比与强变形界面下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性两相流在大粘度对比与强变形界面（薄粘弹性膜/丝）共存时数值鲁棒性不足，需改进界面粘度处理与求解框架。

Method: 采用非结构、几何非分裂VOF框架；界面粘度处理保持几何界面尖锐同时对动量方程粘性贡献正则化；结合质量-动量一致的相平流、可选SIMPLEC压力-速度耦合、含构象张量稳定化的模块化流变框架；以Oldroyd-B和指数Phan-Thien-Tanner模型进行2D、轴对称、3D基准测试。

Result: 计算结果与已发表的数值及实验数据吻合良好，在非结构网格上对苛刻的粘弹性自由表面构型展现出更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DeboRheo v2.0有效提升粘弹性两相流在大粘度对比和强界面变形下的数值稳定性与适用性。

Abstract: We present DeboRheo v2.0, an unstructured, geometrically unsplit Volume-of-Fluid framework implemented in OpenFOAM for viscoelastic two-phase flows. Version 2.0 improves numerical robustness when large viscosity contrasts coincide with strongly deformed interfaces and thin viscoelastic lamellae or filaments. The revised interfacial viscosity treatment retains a sharp geometric interface while regularizing the viscous contribution to the momentum equation. The solver combines mass-momentum-consistent phase advection, optional SIMPLEC pressure-velocity coupling, and a modular rheological framework with conformation-tensor stabilization. We assess the method using two-dimensional, axisymmetric, and three-dimensional benchmarks governed by the Oldroyd-B and exponential Phan-Thien-Tanner models. The results agree well with published numerical and experimental data and demonstrate improved robustness for demanding viscoelastic free-surface configurations on unstructured meshes.

</details>


### [110] [One vertex generates the hard-sphere Boltzmann collision hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17123)
*I. Karlin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: hard-sphere 玻尔兹曼方程的碰撞层级虽为无限维，但其全部多项式碰撞顶点均可由唯一的秩二应力产生顶点 S 通过普适微分与张量运算生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上玻尔兹曼方程的非线性碰撞项被表示为碰撞括号的无限三指标张量，每一多项式阶数似乎都需要新的系数。作者希望揭示这种"无限表示"背后是否存在单一的原初碰撞律，以区分硬球特定物理与普适代数结构。

Method: 以硬球模型为对象，证明整个硬球碰撞张量仅包含一个原初碰撞律——精确的秩二应力产生顶点 S；通过有限的普适微分与张量运算序列从 S 构造任意多项式碰撞顶点，并通过微分得到所有常规碰撞括号。随后在 Mach-5 激波中进行第八阶盲测试。

Result: 第八阶盲测试结果与独立的碰撞采样一致，并强烈放大了微弱的动理学前驱信号。该方法成功从单一标量律生成全部高阶碰撞顶点。

Conclusion: 硬球碰撞层级在维数上是无限的，但其原初碰撞内容仅由一个标量律构成；相互作用物理与其表示可精确分离：所有硬球特定信息集中于 S，而其上的层级为普适代数。

Abstract: The Boltzmann equation is the fundamental kinetic bridge between molecular mechanics and fluid behaviour. It converts binary encounters into the evolution of a velocity distribution, underlies rigorous routes from hard-sphere dynamics to Euler and Navier--Stokes--Fourier equations, and remains indispensable when continuum descriptions fail, from hypersonic shock layers and atmospheric re-entry to rarefied multiscale flows. Yet its nonlinear collision term is conventionally represented by an infinite three-index tensor of collisional brackets, with apparently new coefficients at every polynomial order. Here we prove that this entire hard-sphere tensor contains only one primitive collision law: the exact rank-two stress-production vertex $S$. Every polynomial collision vertex is obtained from $S$ by a finite sequence of universal differential and tensor operations, and every conventional bracket follows by differentiation. Thus the interaction physics and its representation separate exactly: all hard-sphere-specific information is concentrated in $S$, whereas the hierarchy above it is universal algebra. A blind eighth-order test in a Mach-5 shock agrees with independent collision sampling and strongly amplifies a weak kinetic precursor. The hard-sphere hierarchy is infinite in dimension, but its primitive collision content consists of a single scalar law.

</details>


### [111] [Preferential concentration in a periodically forced particle-laden turbulent planar jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17353)
*Mohammad Ahmad,Pierre Bragança,Christophe Cuvier,Martin Obligado*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 低频正弦强迫几乎不改变湍流平面射流的平均流动，却显著改变湍流结构与惯性颗粒的优先聚集；颗粒聚集对强迫相位高度敏感，说明弱时间调制可在不改变平均输运流的前提下控制颗粒组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含颗粒射流中周期强迫很常见，但其对湍流及惯性颗粒聚集的影响尚不清楚，尤其是低频、中等振幅强迫能否在几乎不改变平均流的情况下调控湍流统计和颗粒分布。

Method: 在基于喷口高度的雷诺数约4950与9900的两个湍流平面射流中，施加频率比射流首选不稳定性低三个数量级的正弦强迫；用热线风速仪和高速颗粒成像表征载流湍流，并用Voronoi剖分量化稀相惯性液滴的优先聚集。

Result: 强迫显著改变湍流但平均流发展几乎不变：积分尺度最多减小40%；强迫与未强迫射流的无量纲耗散系数与Taylor尺度雷诺数的时间平均关系保持一致。强迫周期内速度脉动与Taylor微尺度近似反相，使Taylor尺度雷诺数保持在周期均值±10%以内，尽管耗散和Kolmogorov时间尺度变化很大。颗粒聚集更敏感：强迫提高平均优先聚集，并产生占周期均值21%–49%的相位依赖变化；聚集在Kolmogorov时间尺度最大（即颗粒Stokes数最小）时达到峰值，因此在相近的瞬时Taylor尺度雷诺数下可出现显著不同的聚集状态。

Conclusion: 弱时间调制能够控制颗粒组织，而基本不改变平均输运流；这为在相近瞬时湍流尺度雷诺数下实现不同颗粒聚集状态提供了机制依据。

Abstract: Periodic forcing is common in particle-laden jets, yet its influence on turbulence and inertial-particle clustering remains poorly understood. We investigate a turbulent planar jet at two Reynolds numbers, approximately 4950 and 9900 based on nozzle height, subjected to sinusoidal forcing at a frequency three orders of magnitude below the preferred jet instability. Hot-wire anemometry and high-speed particle imaging characterise the carrier turbulence, while Voronoi tessellations quantify preferential concentration of dilute inertial droplets.
  Despite its low frequency and moderate amplitude, forcing substantially modifies turbulence while leaving the mean-flow development nearly unchanged. The integral scale decreases by up to $40\%$, while forced and unforced jets retain the same time-averaged relationship between the dimensionless dissipation coefficient and Taylor-scale Reynolds number. Within the forcing cycle, velocity fluctuations and the Taylor microscale vary approximately in opposite phase, keeping the Taylor-scale Reynolds number within $\pm10\%$ of its cycle mean despite substantial variations in dissipation and Kolmogorov time scale.
  Particle clustering is even more sensitive, with forcing increasing mean preferential concentration and producing phase-dependent variations of $21$--$49\%$ of the cycle mean. Clustering peaks when the Kolmogorov time scale is largest, corresponding to the smallest particle Stokes number. Thus, markedly different clustering states occur at similar instantaneous Taylor-scale Reynolds numbers. These findings demonstrate that weak temporal modulation can control particle organisation without substantially altering the mean transporting flow.

</details>


### [112] [A complete forward projection algorithm through cylindrical and spherical refractive windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17413)
*Shijie Zhong,Shiyong Tan,Xiaosi Zhang,Yanying Li,Rui Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用光线守恒动量将柱面/球面窗口下的三维点投影化简为具有凹结构的闭式方程，可判定单/多投影并精确计算其像素位置，实现了迭代方法难以获得的重投影，且基于此的标定达到图像噪声极限，用于圆柱窗口下旋流场的三维粒子追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验与工程中常通过圆柱或球面窗口进行观测，折射导致三维点到像素的投影计算困难：一个三维点可能存在多个投影像，现有投影算法难以有效判定与求解。因此需要一种能预测可见性与多像位置的高效投影方法，以支撑管道流测量、水下重建等应用。

Method: 借助光线在折射系统中的守恒动量，将柱面/球面窗口下的投影问题化简为具有凹结构的闭式方程，从而判定三维点是否存在单一投影或多重投影，并求出相应的投影像位置；进一步将该算法用于棒状（wand）标定，并应用于圆柱窗口内旋流场的三维粒子追踪。

Result: 仿真中，单次搜索即可恢复迭代法即使多次重启仍遗漏的像；基于该方法的标定精度可达图像噪声所限定的精度极限；在圆柱窗口下的旋流场中实现了准确的三维粒子追踪。

Conclusion: 该算法为曲面窗口下的可见性与多重成像预测提供了有效手段，对三维管道流测量、高精度水下重建等实际应用具有启示意义。

Abstract: Cylindrical or spherical windows often appear in experiments and real applications. Measuring through those windows requires projecting 3D points to pixels, which is different to compute due to refraction. A point may have multiple projected images but cannot be efficiently determine with the existing projection algorithms. Using the ray's conserved momenta, we reduce this projection to closed-form equations with a concave structure. As a result, we can successfully determine whether a 3D point has a single projection or multiple and compute those locations. In simulations, with one single search, we can recover images that iterative methods miss even with many restarts. A wand calibration based on it can reach the precision limit set by image noise, enabling accurate 3D particle tracking in a swirling flow through cylinder windows. This algorithm shows how to predict visibility and multiple images through curved windows, shedding light for 3D pipe flow measurements, accurate underwater reconstruction, and many real applications.

</details>


### [113] [Gas radiation and the gravity limit of flicker in a co-flowing hydrogen jet diffusion flame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17595)
*Tianfang Xie,Meng Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 辐射通过“热气层浮力损失”和“位移-辐射阻尼”两种机制抑制同流氢气射流扩散火焰的振荡，据此建立了以辐射数为参数的闪烁临界判据，并预测了临界重力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规重力下气体辐射作用较弱，但在微重力下浮力减弱而辐射不变，因此辐射成为决定火焰闪烁（振荡）能否维持的关键因素；需要厘清辐射停止闪烁的机制并给出可预测的临界条件。

Method: 采用基于火焰面（flamelet）的数值模拟，取Lewis数为1，与飞秒相干反斯托克斯拉曼散射测量的闪烁频率（误差4%以内）对比验证；结合局部稳定性分析，通过开关辐射源、冻结辐射源等对照算例分离辐射的响应机制，并分析扰动向上游传播速度与同流竞争的关系。

Result: 在0.1倍常规重力下，绝热火焰闪烁而辐射火焰不闪烁，关闭辐射即恢复闪烁；冻结辐射源（仅去掉其对扰动的响应）使振荡下游衰退速度减慢十倍以上。无辐射时扰动传播速度随重力平方根标度，辐射使其降低但不再满足该标度。临界条件为同流Froude数平方根与辐射数（浮力与辐射时间尺度之比）的线性关系；标定后给出实验同流条件下临界重力为0.13倍常规重力，为绝热值的2.6倍，并正确预测了另外两个火焰的闪烁与否。超过临界值时，瞬态波包可在短时落塔实验中伪装成闪烁。

Conclusion: 气体辐射在低重力下通过浮力损失与位移-辐射阻尼两个不随重力减弱的机制抑制火焰闪烁；闪烁极限由辐射数与同流Froude数平方根构成的线性判据描述，该判据具备跨工况预测能力，但需注意落塔瞬态波包可能造成误判。

Abstract: Although weak at normal gravity, gas radiation sets the gravity at which a co-flowing hydrogen jet diffusion flame stops flickering. Flamelet-based simulations with unity Lewis numbers, which reproduce the flicker frequency measured by femtosecond coherent anti-Stokes Raman scattering within 4%, are combined with local stability analysis. At one tenth of normal gravity, the adiabatic flame flickers, the radiating flame does not, and switching the emission off restores flicker. Radiation acts through two mechanisms that do not weaken at reduced gravity. The hot outer layer loses buoyancy in proportion to the gas residence time, and a disturbance that displaces the flame also displaces its emission, which damps the disturbance on the lean flank (displaced-emission damping). Freezing the radiative source, which removes only its response to disturbances, slows the downstream recession of the oscillation more than tenfold. Both mechanisms slow the upstream spreading of disturbances. This spreading, competing with the co-flow, sets the onset location and the critical co-flow. Without radiation, the spreading speed scales with the square root of gravity; its radiative reduction does not. At reduced gravity, the flicker limit therefore becomes a straight line relating the square root of the co-flow Froude number to a radiation number, the ratio of buoyant to radiative time scales. The calibrated line places the critical gravity for the experimental co-flow at 0.13 times normal gravity, 2.6 times the adiabatic value. It correctly predicted whether two further flames keep flickering. Beyond the limit, transient wave packets can mimic flicker throughout a short drop-tower test.

</details>


### [114] [High-fidelity Three-Dimensional Flow Field Reconstruction Driven by Turbulent Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18045)
*Hanlin Kong,Jianan Wei,Yuntian Chen,Yujia Wang,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种湍流感知框架，用于从粗粒化数据重建时间分辨的三维流场，结合多尺度神经算子、状态空间模型、Navier-Stokes残差软约束与专家混合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真三维湍流流场对科学与工程分析至关重要，但直接数值模拟或高分辨率测量成本高昂。基于粗粒化数据的超分辨率重建是一种有前景的替代方案，但细尺度结构本质上是三维的且跨尺度动态耦合，现有数据驱动方法难以恢复多尺度体积结构及其相干时间演化。

Method: 该框架将用于恢复体积结构的多尺度神经算子与促进时间一致性的状态空间模型相耦合；引入Navier-Stokes残差作为软约束以正则化重建，使其趋向物理合理的动力学；并采用专家混合（MoE）机制支持对未知粗粒化方式的无监督自适应。

Result: 在典型湍流系统和大气再分析数据上的评估表明，该框架相比基于学习和经典基线降低了重建误差，同时更好地保持了多尺度空间结构与相干时间演化。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该框架从粗粒化数据重建高保真三维流场的有效性。

Abstract: High-fidelity three-dimensional turbulent flow fields are essential for scientific and engineering analyses, yet obtaining them through direct numerical simulation or high-resolution measurements remains expensive. Super-resolution reconstruction from coarse-grained data therefore offers a promising alternative, but remains fundamentally challenging because fine-scale structures are intrinsically three-dimensional and dynamically coupled across scales. Existing data-driven approaches still struggle to recover multiscale volumetric structures and their coherent temporal evolution. To address these limitations, we propose a turbulence-aware framework for time-resolved three-dimensional flow reconstruction. The framework couples a multiscale neural operator, designed to recover volumetric structures, with a state space model to promote temporal consistency in the reconstructed dynamics. In addition, Navier-Stokes residuals are incorporated as soft constraints to regularize the reconstruction toward physically plausible dynamics. A mixture-of-experts mechanism is also introduced to support unsupervised adaptation to unknown coarse-graining. Across evaluations on canonical turbulent systems and atmospheric reanalysis data, the proposed framework reduces reconstruction errors relative to learning-based and classical baselines, while better preserving multiscale spatial structure and coherent temporal evolution. These results validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in reconstructing high-fidelity three-dimensional flow fields from coarse-grained data.

</details>


### [115] [One-way pressure-independent valves with hysteresis and chattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18242)
*Fabio Bagagiolo,Andrea Corli,Stefan Moreti,Massimiliano D. Rosini*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一维等温气体经阀门流动的守恒律模型，研究含非经典激波的黎曼问题，刻画阀门颤振与滞后稳定化两种振荡行为及其参数依赖与相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阀门通过启闭控制出流处于指定区间，其开闭依赖另一参数，会产生颤振与滞后两类振荡现象，需建立简洁模型刻画其机理及相互关系。

Method: 建立由两个守恒律组成、并带耦合进出口流条件的系统；求解相应黎曼问题，处理出现的非经典激波；通过参数分析与数值模拟研究颤振与滞后。

Result: 刻画了导致颤振的状态，给出其依赖模型参数的方式；揭示了颤振（数值模拟中尤为明显）与阀门操作诱导的滞后稳定化之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该模型虽简单，却能有效刻画阀门流动中颤振与滞后稳定化两类关键振荡行为，并阐明其参数依赖与相互影响。

Abstract: We propose a model for the one-dimensional isothermal gas flow through a valve in a pipe. The valve aims at keeping the outgoing flow in an assigned interval; moreover, its opening and closing depend on a further parameter. The model is realized by a system of two conservation laws with suitable conditions coupling the ingoing and the outgoing flow. We study the corresponding Riemann problem, where non-classical shocks show up. Although simple, the model allows to study two key oscillatory behaviors of the flow, which are due, respectively, to the chattering of the valve, especially seen in the numerical simulations, and to the stabilizing hysteresis induced by the valve operation. We characterize the states leading to chattering and show how they depend on the parameters of the model. Through several numerical simulations, we study the interplay between the two above phenomena.

</details>


### [116] [Partially-Averaged Navier-Stokes simulation of turbulent annular Poiseulle flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18254)
*Li-Toong Yap,Marten Klein,Heiko Schmidt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用PANS模型对$Re_{D_h}=8900$的湍流环形Poiseuille流动进行模拟，验证其在捕捉横向壁面曲率效应方面与DNS及RANS类尺度解析方法相当，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流环形Poiseuille流动广泛存在于换热器等工程场景，内壁横向曲率（由内外半径比$\eta=R_i/R_o$表征）对流场的影响此前多依赖DNS研究。基于RANS框架的尺度解析模拟相比LES更经济，但其能否捕捉环形管流的湍流各向异性尚不明确。

Method: 在$Re_{D_h}=8900$条件下，采用部分平均Navier-Stokes（PANS）模型——一种基于传统RANS建模的可变分辨率湍流模型——对湍流环形Poiseuille流动进行模拟。

Result: PANS模型捕捉横向壁面曲率效应的能力与DNS及其他基于RANS的尺度解析方法相当，尽管其相对简单且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: PANS模型可作为DNS与LES之外一种经济有效的替代方案，用于模拟湍流环形Poiseuille流动中的壁面曲率效应。

Abstract: Turbulent annular Poiseulle flow is commonly encountered in various engineering applications, such as heat exchangers. The effects of the inner wall transverse curvature on the flowfield, characterised by the ratio of inner and outer radii $η= R_{i}/R_{o}$, have been studied previously using Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS). Scale-resolving simulations based on a Reynolds-Averaged Navier Stokes framework offer a potentially cheaper alternative to traditional Large Eddy Simulations (LES); however it is not clear if such models are able to capture the turbulence anisotropy in the turbulent annular pipe flow. In the present study, turbulent annular Poiseulle flow at $Re_{D_{h}} = 8900$ is performed with the Partially-Averaged Navier Stokes (PANS) model, a variable-resolution turbulence model that is based on the conventional RANS modelling. The ability of the PANS model to capture the effects of the transverse wall curvature is found to be comparable with DNS and other RANS-based scale-resolving approaches despite its relative simplicity and reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [117] [Preferential concentration of cloud droplets: Coupled effects of phase change and gravitational settling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18255)
*Zi-Mo Liao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究建立了量化液滴惯性、重力沉降与相变耦合效应的解析理论，发现在二维均匀热对流中相变通过沉降速度的空间差异显著增强云滴的优先聚集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云滴在湍流中受有限惯性、重力沉降及凝结/蒸发导致的粒径变化共同作用，其在湍流中的非均匀累积（优先聚集）会影响液滴碰撞与合并速率，因此需要量化惯性、沉降与相变三者耦合对浓度变化的影响。

Method: 发展了一套解析理论，用于刻画液滴惯性、重力沉降与相变对浓度变化的耦合效应，并将其应用于二维均匀热对流的数值模拟中进行分析。

Result: 相变通过沉降速度的空间差异显著增强优先聚集：液滴沿轨迹经历不同热力学历史而产生不同粒径；液滴输运通过重新排列不同尺寸液滴进一步增大沉降贡献；流体变形则通过稀释浓度结构部分抵消该增强效应，降低了固定空间尺度上测得的优先聚集。

Conclusion: 相变是本流动中优先聚集的重要增强机制，其作用主要通过粒径差异引起的沉降速度空间差异实现，并受液滴输运与流体变形的共同调制。

Abstract: Cloud droplets respond to turbulent motion with finite inertia and settle under gravity, while condensation and evaporation change their sizes. The nonuniform accumulation of cloud droplets in turbulent flows, known as preferential concentration, has attracted sustained interest because it affects droplet collision and coalescence rates. We develop an analytical theory that quantifies the coupled effects of droplet inertia, gravitational settling, and phase change on concentration changes. Applying this theory to simulations of two-dimensional homogeneous thermal convection reveals that phase change markedly enhances preferential concentration in this flow, primarily through spatial differences in settling speed. These differences arise because droplets experience different thermodynamic histories along their trajectories and thus acquire different sizes. Droplet transport further increases this settling contribution by rearranging droplets of different sizes. Fluid deformation partly offsets this enhancement by thinning concentration structures, reducing the preferential concentration measured at a fixed spatial scale.

</details>


### [118] [Some generalizations of the tornado model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18663)
*S. N. Artekha*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文基于Schwarzschild对流不稳定判据，构建了龙卷风生成的三层（简化、精细化、完整）流体力学模型，通过解析与数值求解刻画旋转对流从初始到准稳态的发展，讨论单涡与双眼龙卷的结构特征及生成判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龙卷风生成机制尚缺乏统一的理论描述，作者试图用对流不稳定（Schwarzschild判据）与旋转对流框架解释涡旋从初始扰动到准稳态的完整发展过程。

Method: 建立含流函数方程、方位速度分量方程与密度变化方程的完整方程组；提出精细化模型（理想流体框架下的旋转对流）与最简模型（仅含流函数方程，适用于涡旋生成初始阶段）。最简模型给出由8个随高度线性关系组合的解析解；完整模型对旋转对流全过程进行数值研究，考虑单涡与双眼龙卷两种情形。

Result: 最简模型：过冷云-气混合体沿轴下沉、超绝热温度梯度使近地面过热气团上升；旋转因角动量守恒而增强。精细化模型：出现单向差动旋转，且依赖边界条件（可为局部的）。完整模型：各速度分量协同增长；近地面径向流汇向中心，龙卷顶部“壁”处气流沿轴向与外围流出；最大垂直速度出现在某一径向距离和高度处，最大方位速度出现在龙卷“壁”内。

Conclusion: 旋转对流模型可自洽描述龙卷风从生成到准稳态的发展，并给出速度场结构与最大速度位置特征；文中进一步讨论了龙卷生成的判据。

Abstract: A possible mechanism of tornado generation is associated with the Schwarzschild criterion for convective instability. The supercooled cloud-air mixture near the axis starts to descend and, due to the super adiabatic temperature gradient, the overheated near-surface air mass rises. The complete system of equations for full model includes equations for the stream function, for the azimuthal velocity component, and for the change in density. The refined model considers rotating convection within the framework of ideal hydrodynamics. The simplest model uses a separate equation for the stream function and can be applied to the initial stage of vortex generation. Within the framework of the simplest model, an analytical solution is combined from 8 linear dependencies by height. Rotation increases due to the conservation of angular momentum. Within the framework of the refined model, some unidirectional differential rotation arises, but it depends on boundary conditions (can be local). The full model studies the tornado development as rotating convection from the very beginning to the quasi-stationary stage. Velocity components increase in a coordinated manner. Two cases are considered: a single whirlwind and a two-eyed tornado. Radial flows converge towards the central part of the tornado near the earth surface, and there is an outflow from the "wall" to the axis and to the periphery near the top of the tornado. The maximum vertical velocity component is achieved at a certain radial distance at a certain height. The maximum azimuthal velocity component is achieved in the tornado "wall". Tornado generation criteria are discussed.

</details>
